# ios_liquid_glass 2B.3: press, Reduce Motion everywhere, frame cost — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking. A line `RUN[label]: \`command\` => PASS|FAIL` is a command to run from the repository root with that outcome; the "Expected output ends with" block after it is the command's last lines (timings aside).

**Goal.** Finish project 2B (spec §4, 2B.3): give the lab the glow and stretch measures (L4) and a take check that keeps squeezed recordings out of them, record native's drags, neighbours, Operator's sizes and Reduce Motion presses (N3, N4, N6), make `Glass.interactive()` press like native interactive glass (M7), make Reduce Motion everywhere follow native (M8), keep animated glass within 20 % of still glass (M10), document the API (M9), move Operator's press onto the package (the user's decision D9), and verify against spec §8 "2B.3 is done when" in `docs/liquid_glass/02b-motion/results-2b3.md`.

**Architecture.**
- **Lab.** `press.py` reads, per touch step and per tracked glass, the rows `shapes.capture` already makes plus one new value, `glow`: the mean luma inside the glass's rest box (or a pinned rect). From them it derives thirteen press measures (`press.glow_peak`, `glow_rise_ms`, `glow_fade_ms`, `glow_drop`, `glow_drop_ms`, `glow_level`, `glow_half_ms`, `grow_w`, `grow_h`, `grow_ms`, `shrink_ms`, `stretch`, `centre`), judged at max(fixed threshold, 1.5 × noise). `takecheck.py` finds a take's capture holes, squeezed presses and missing touches; `run` re-records such a take and `repeat` keeps it out of every floor. `pressfit.py` fits the package's press numbers from clean native series and writes `ios27_press.dart`; nothing is typed.
- **Package.** Each `GlassMember` owns a `GlassPress`: a growth spring, a swell spring for the light under the finger, a focus that follows the finger, and an even "hold" light that goes in one frame at a time that depends on the glass's size. The coordinator samples it on its ticker with the member's other springs, so a press rebuilds no widget. The member's drawn rect and its content scale together about the centre by the size law. An interactive `GlassEffect` puts a `GlassPressTarget` (raw pointer events, hit-tested against the glass's shape, never in the gesture arena) around its content and paints the light with `GlassPressGlow` under the content.
- **Example.** The N2 sizes, Operator's two sizes, the N3 drags and N4 neighbours as live scenes with native's tap ids; `perf.motion` and `perf.slide`.
- **Operator.** `GlassSurface(pressable: true)` becomes `Glass.interactive()`; `_PressLift` and its `GlassGlow` go (Task 10, separable, D9).

**Tech stack.** Flutter 3.44.5 / Dart, Impeller, `motor` springs through `GlassAnimation`; Python 3 (stdlib, Pillow, numpy) for the harness; Xcode 27, SwiftUI and XCUITest; the iOS 27 simulator.

**Spec.** `docs/liquid_glass/02b-motion/spec.md` (approved 2026-10-03): §4 (2B.3), §5 L4, §6 N3, N4, N6, §7 M7–M10, §8 "2B.3 is done when", §9, §12. Read `docs/liquid_glass/ROADMAP.md` §3–§5 and gotchas 35 and 43–52 first, then the spec, `02b-motion/brainstorm.md` (section 4 as revised to (a2)), `spike-interactive.md`, `results-2b2.md`, `todo-2b2.md`, `todo-2b1.md` and this plan's review and re-review, `02b-motion/plan-2b3-review.md` (F1–F21) and `02b-motion/rereview-2b3.md` (R1–R10), answered below.

**Scope of the prototype.** The prototype answered the questions this plan cannot be written without, with a few native takes each, and ran the package's press against native on every case of the six 2B.1 press sizes (Flutter recorded on 2026-10-09 with the refitted table, judged against the archive's N1 and N2 references, which the fit read, and against the 18 clean sampled 2B.1 noise takes, which it did not) and on the two held-out sizes, the 44 × 44 circle and the 250 × 150 rect. It recorded **no noise floor** for any press measure: every prototype count below is at the fixed thresholds only, and Task 13 makes the floors. The full N3, N4 and N6 reference sets are execution work (Task 12).

**Rulings the prototype forced, with evidence.** Each refines or departs from the spec; where they differ, these win. Run folders are the prototype's (`build/glass_lab/runs/`); `R` is `docs/liquid_glass/02b-motion/research/proto-2b3`.

*Lab (spec L4)*

1. **The press measures are read per touch step on the glass's rest box** (Task 1, `press.py`, tests `test_press.py`). For each touch step of a scene and each tracked glass: the rest is the last frame before touch-down; `glow` is the mean luma inside the rest box (the box `track.box_pixels` finds in `ready.png` against the bare frame) or inside the region's pinned rect (ruling 4); `glow_peak` is its largest rise during the touch; `glow_rise_ms` the time from touch-down to 90 % of that peak; `glow_fade_ms` the time from touch-up until it stays within 10 % of the peak; `glow_drop` the largest fall between two consecutive frames after touch-up, `glow_drop_ms` when it happens and `glow_level` the light just before it (the level the fade reached, F13); `glow_half_ms` the time from touch-up to halfway between the peak and that level (or rest, without a cut), so the fade itself is judged and not only the cut (F13: `glow_fade_ms` equals `glow_drop_ms` whenever there is a cut); `grow_w` and `grow_h` the largest growth of the box during the touch; `grow_ms` the time to 90 % of `grow_w`; `shrink_ms` the time from touch-up until the width stays within max(10 % of `grow_w`, 2/3 pt); `stretch` the signed largest change of width/height against rest from touch-down to the next touch; `centre` the largest move of the box centre in that span. Thresholds: luminance 3 for `glow_peak`, `glow_drop` and `glow_level`, 17 ms for the times, 1 pt (`bbox_pt`) for `grow_w`/`grow_h`, a new `ratio` 0.01 for `stretch`, 1 pt (`centre_pt`) for `centre` (spec L4's numbers). **A shape that does not light (peak under 3 luma) or grow (under 1 pt) has no time measures; both apps without one agree (0), one app without one fails (inf).** That is how "absence where native shows none" is judged (spec §8 2B.3 item 2). **It reproduces the spike** (`R/reproduce-glow.txt`, `reproduce.py glow`): v13 dark-stripes first press: rise 35.0 ms (spike 35), fade 403.3 ms (spike 403), growth +12.0 / +4.67 pt (spike +12 / +4.67), growth to 90 % 210 ms (spike 210); the one-frame drop 11.23 luma at 403.3 ms; light-photo luma +19.00 / +19.05 (spike +19.04); N1 dark-photo +20.61 with its drop 11.39 at 400.0 ms.
2. **`shrink_ms` reads the release undershoot the spike could not see**, so it differs from the spike's release times: v13 dark-stripes 273 ms against the spike's 148 (the spike's Δw "cannot see the glass shrinking below rest", `spike-interactive.md` Limits; N1 dark-photo is back at its rest width of 251.33 pt 161 ms after release, 250.33 pt at 215 ms and 251.33 pt again at 361 ms).

*Native answers (spec N3, N4, decision a2)*

3. **Native never releases a press while the finger is down, at any distance recorded, and it does not stretch on a drag inside the glass; past the edge it leans toward the finger** (`R/native-questions.txt`, `R/native-questions-2.txt`; clean takes are named, F10). Fast drag inside the glass (+60 pt at 800 pt/s, `material.drag.fast`, `20261009-055621`, holed but its sizes unaffected): `stretch` 0.007, `centre` 0.24 pt, within a plain press's 0.002–0.017 and 0.17–0.60 pt. Past the edge, the glass stays grown and lit until touch-up and then releases, every time: 40 pt to the right (`material.drag.out`, `20261009-055711`), 106 pt above the top edge (`material.drag.up`, clean `20261009-084601` and `-084726`) and **256 pt below the bottom edge** (`material.drag.far`, +300 pt down, clean `20261009-084517`, agreeing with the other take `-084254`: width 263 → 258 pt and height 92.7 → 94.3 pt while the finger moves away, centre 1.67 pt toward it, then the release 3.3 s after touch-down at touch-up). So **a drag that leaves the glass does not release it, against spec M7's "a drag that leaves the glass ... releases with the release spring"**; the package keeps the press until touch-up (test `a drag that leaves the glass keeps the press until the finger lifts, as native does`). Native's lean toward a finger outside the glass is measured but **not built**: `stretch` +0.012 at 40 pt right, −0.015 at 106 pt up, −0.035 at 256 pt down; `centre` 1.33, 0.83–0.85 and 1.67 pt; one or two takes per distance, not monotonic in it (D6). The package's glass keeps its shape, so `material.drag.{out,up,far}` are forecast to fail `stretch` and `centre`. During a drag native's light dims and holds (+20.6 → +17.1 to +17.3 luma, `drag.out` +16.2); the package's light follows the finger (ruling 13).
4. **Native lights and scales only the pressed glass; its neighbour neither lights nor scales, touching, merged or apart** (`material.neighbours.0`, `.10`, `.40`, `20261009-055759`, `-055858`, `-055951`, holed at step 1, which moves times, not these): the neighbour's glow peak 0.39, 0.02 and 0.004 luma against the pressed glass's 23.5–23.9, and its far edge does not move (`xmax` 321.67, 326.67 and 341.67 pt throughout). So **the container-level glow layer of spec M7 is not built**: it exists only so that light can reach neighbours, and native shows none (decision a2). Each glass paints its own light, clipped to itself. **How the neighbour is judged (F8):** its region and a pinned light rect (64 × 64 pt) start 32 pt past its facing edge, the law's growth plus `spacing`, so the pressed glass and the merge bridge never enter them: in the first round the neighbour's region took the bridge for growth (`grow_w` 5.67 pt at gap 10) and its detected rest box on dark `photo` covered 56.67–67.67 of its 88 pt. The **pressed** glass's size measures at gaps 0 and 10 still include the bridge, and are forecast as merge-model failures (todo-2b2's neck and timing rows), not press.
5. **The content scales with the glass** (`R/content_scale.py` on the spike's labelled glass, archive runs `P-20261003-012604` v18 and `P-20261003-012517` v17): v18's "Glass" label 65.0 × 20.33 → 69.33 × 21.67 pt (× 1.067 / × 1.066, the glass × 1.07), v17's 65.0 × 20.33 → 72.33 × 23.0 (× 1.113 / × 1.131, the glass × 1.12). The press is one uniform scale of glass and content about the glass's centre.

*The press model (spec M7; every number from `pressfit.py`, `R/pressfit.json`, on `R/native-press-all.json` (N1 and N2, every case, 20 of 24 takes clean under ruling 16's rule) and `R/native-questions-2.json` (one clean normal and six clean Reduce Motion `material.interactive` takes, and the question takes, which no fit but the law's plateaus reads), and `R/glow-profile.json`)*

6. **The light is two parts, and it is white where native's is not** (`glass_press.dart`, `glass_press_widgets.dart`): an even "hold" light over the whole glass and a "swell" under the finger, a Gaussian of sigma 77 pt. Sigma is fitted **inside the capsule only** (F4: the first round's 61 pt came from rings that took in the empty corners of the bounding box) on the ring profile of native Reduce Motion presses (no growth): the median, ring by ring, of the six clean takes `20261009-085013` … `-085628`, each read at its own hold level (R8; `pressfit.py profile`, `R/glow-profile.json`); RMS 1.59 luma over 126 rings. Per size, the hold is 9.8–11.3 luma (0 at 360 × 200 and 300 × 250) and the swell's centre 7.9–32.6 luma (the 250 × 150 and 300 × 250 rows from one take each), medians over all four backdrops (F9). Evidence for two parts: after release native's light decays to a level and drops by that level in one frame, and under Reduce Motion it decays to 11.6 and holds there 140 ms before the drop. **Native's light is not a white overlay: solid on luma, suggestive only on colour** (`R/light_blend.py`, `R/light-blend.txt`, the hold isolated at its one-frame cut on the four N1 cases). Solid: the cut's luma step is 12.5, 12.4 and 12.3 on dark `photo`, dark `stripes` and light `photo`, and **halves on light `stripes` (6.8)**; one white overlay cannot hold its strength on light `photo` and halve on light `stripes`. Suggestive only: by channel the change is near-neutral on `photo` (dark R +15.5, G +11.9, B +9.9) and green-heavy on `stripes` (light R −2.7, G +10.4, B −1.3), but the recordings are H.264 with 4:2:0 chroma, so colour at the stripes' scale is not resolved and no colour claim rests on it. **The light's model is chosen held out** (R5): each model fitted on three of the four cases and judged on the fourth, RMS over pixels and channels, mean of the four: an affine map of the glass's luma and chroma (brightness and saturation) 6.14, a constant added light (white, the package's) 9.51, a share of the headroom 255 − c (screen) 12.45. The package adds white, so **`glow_peak` is forecast to fail on light `stripes`** (the first-round review measured 3.48) and is class (b), D10.
7. **The light goes in one frame at a time that depends on the glass's size** (F5): median 651.7 ms after release for the 58 pt circle, 525 (138 × 53), 418.3 (250 × 44), 400 (250 × 88), 380.8 (300 × 120) and 371.7 (250 × 150, one take; 360 × 200 and 300 × 250 have no cut and take it), interpolated in log area like the hold and swell; under Reduce Motion 564.2 ms, from six clean presses (F6: `pressfit` refuses fewer than five). After a press-drag native cuts later (526.7–650 ms after release on `material.interactive` step 3, 540–563 ms on the clean drag takes); why is **not known**, so the press-drag steps are forecast to fail `glow_drop_ms` (Flutter cuts at the press time).
8. **The growth law is fitted on the settled growth of every take, as a function of width and height, and its form must keep every measured size within the size threshold** (F3, R4, S2, D12): the first round fitted the peak and then drove a spring that overshoots it by 8.6 %, counting the overshoot twice; the law reads the plateau (the last 0.3 s of the touch) of **every** normal `photo` take, holed or not, 15 takes over 8 sizes, the 250 × 150 and 300 × 250 rects included: a hole moves times, and the plateau is read 0.3 s before touch-up, where none of the holes found lies (`pressfit.plateaus`; test `test_the_law_reads_every_take_s_plateau_holed_or_not`); the timing fits still skip holed takes. **Three capped forms are fitted** (`pressfit.law_fit`): *height*, min(cap, a × (100 / h)^p); *area*, min(cap, a × (10⁴ / (w × h))^p); and *height and width*, min(cap, a × (100 / h)^p × (100 / w)^q) with q from −1 to 1, which also contains the settled-scale form 1 + c / (w × h)^p (q = p − 1). **The form is chosen in two steps** (`pressfit.fit_growth_law`): only a form whose median plateau at **every** measured size is within the 1 pt size threshold (`bbox_pt`, the `grow_w` threshold) qualifies, because spec M7 and Done item 1 ask for native's law at every size; then the lower leave-one-size-out RMS chooses among those (alone, leave-one-size-out over-weights the three 88 pt takes and preferred a law that missed two heights, the second re-review's S2). `pressfit` refuses to write when no form qualifies, and says when the qualifying forms are not separated (held-out RMS within 1/3 pt of each other). Tests: `test_a_height_law_is_recovered_with_its_cap_and_no_width_term`, `test_a_law_in_width_and_height_is_recovered_on_portrait_and_landscape_sizes`, `test_the_law_is_fitted_on_the_settled_growth_so_a_spring_overshoot_is_not_counted_twice`, `test_a_form_that_misses_a_measured_size_by_more_than_the_size_threshold_is_not_chosen`, `test_problems_name_a_law_with_a_height_outside_the_size_threshold`. **Result** (`R/pressfit.json`): the height form, **min(15.84, 9.73 × (100 / h)^1.24) pt**, with residuals −0.16 (58 × 58), +0.51 (138 × 53), −0.33 (250 × 44), +0.07 (250 × 88), −0.41 (300 × 120), +0.55 (250 × 150), +0.45 (360 × 200) and −0.88 pt (300 × 250), in-sample RMS 0.442, leave-one-size-out 0.698. The height-and-width form also qualifies, with q = −0.10 (wider glass grows very slightly more), leave-one-size-out 0.789: **the data cannot separate it from the height form** (0.09 pt apart), and the width adds nothing measurable at these sizes, so the simpler form is chosen. The area form fails 250 × 150 by +1.66 pt. The second round's observation that 300 × 250 grows more than the shorter 360 × 200 is within these residuals (−0.88 and +0.45). Earlier rounds: the first chose a power 1.22 on 11 clean takes, the second kept the inverse law on 13 by a 1/3 pt held-out-gain rule, the third a power 1.31 on 14 heights that the held-out 300 × 250 then missed by 1.08 pt; that size is now fitted. Two sizes are **held out** and judged, each one clean native take against one Flutter take recorded with this table, on dark `photo`, at fixed thresholds (`R/heldout.txt`): the 44 × 44 circle passes 10 of 15 (native `20261009-103129`, Flutter `20261014-165002`): its growth within 0.33 pt (native 16.67 × 17.33 pt, Flutter 17.0 × 17.33, at the cap), its light 10.3 luma dimmer (peak 53.5 against 43.2), its cut 83 ms early (750 against 666.7 ms), its fade's half 27 ms late and its release 85 ms faster (`shrink_ms` 250 against 335). **The 120 × 250 portrait capsule** (native `20261014-164610`, Flutter `20261014-164826`) was chosen to separate width from height: it is the size where the three forms disagree most (height 3.12 pt, height and width 2.81, area 8.57), it swaps the roles of the sides (no fitted size is portrait), and it is neither capped nor near a fitted size. It passes 6 of 16, and **the law misses it**: native settles at 4.67 pt wide and 8.0 pt tall, the law gives 3.12 pt in width (−1.55 pt), and Flutter settles at 3.33 × 6.67 (`grow_w` and `grow_h` both 1.67 pt under native's peaks of 5.0 × 9.0); `shrink_ms` (153 ms), `glow_rise_ms`, `grow_ms`, `delay_ms`, `glow_peak` (3.37 luma) and `stretch` (0.012) also fail. So no fitted form gives native's growth for portrait glass: the height form under-grows it and the area form over-grows it. Not adopted, because it would be tuned on the held-out size: the portrait's numbers match the height law with the shorter side in place of the height, growing the longer side (the law gives 7.7 pt at 120 pt, native's height grows 8.0; 300 × 120's width grows 8.17), which the user left to execution (D13).
9. **Springs and delays** (medians over 23 clean takes): growth starts 51.3 ms after touch-down (the first visible change, 1/3 pt, minus the time the fitted spring takes to reach it) on a spring of 0.27 s / 0.62; release starts 20.7 ms after touch-up on 0.33 s / 0.59. The light rises from 0 on an overdamped 0.04 s / 1.845 spring, holds 83.3 ms after release, and the swell fades on 0.31 s / 1.35. The spring grid reaches 0.01 s and damping 4 (F6); 6.9 % of the per-take fits sit on its edge, under `pressfit`'s 25 % limit.

*Reduce Motion (spec M8, N6)*

10. **Under Reduce Motion native's press lights as usual and never grows** (`material.interactive` with `--a11y reduce-motion`, six clean takes `20261009-085013` … `-085628`): glow peak 19.6 (normal 20.6), `grow_w` 0, `grow_h` 0.33 pt; the cut 560–570 ms after release. Spec M8's starting guess ("press: brightens only, with no growth, overshoot or stretch") holds, with a later cut. So `GlassGlow` keeps its glow under Reduce Motion, and `LiquidStretch` does nothing under it (Task 6). Whether the swell still follows the finger under Reduce Motion is **not measured**. Materialize, merge and morph under Reduce Motion are 2B.1's and 2B.2's, carried with their failures (Done item 3).

*Touches (spec M7, §9)*

11. **The press target** (`RenderGlassPressTarget`): raw pointer events, so a `TextButton` inside interactive glass keeps its tap; hit-tested against the glass's shape at its layout, with `HitTestBehavior.opaque` (a touch in a capsule's corner does not press, and inside the shape the glass takes touches meant for widgets behind it, said in the README, F19); the first target to see a pointer down claims it and a hit-test path is dispatched innermost first, so only the innermost nested interactive glass presses, and a second finger is ignored; it releases on pointer up or cancel, when it is disposed (a glass removed or turned plain mid-press), and **when the nearest scroll view's offset changes while the finger is down**. A lone drag recognizer in a `ListView` wins the arena at pointer down and starts a drag activity at once, so `ScrollPosition.isScrollingNotifier` would release every press immediately. **No native recording of a scroll taking over a press exists**: that release is spec M7's design. The target carries a `GlobalKey`, so a press that starts while the glass materializes survives the glass's subtree being rebuilt when it settles. Tests: the eleven touch and lifecycle tests of `glass_press_test.dart`, five of them added for F19 (a standalone glass removed mid-press, a Reduce Motion switch mid-press applying from the next press, a grown container glass removed, a second finger, `withGlassAnimation` leaving the press's springs alone).
12. **A touch's time is the first frame after the pointer event.** Pointer events arrive between frames, when the coordinator has no frame time; stamping them with the last sampled time started the growth at once and skipped its delay. The test `a press lights the glass at once and grows it after the growth delay, uniformly, to the size law` checks that 48 ms after the touch the glass has not grown. (The first round cited run `20261009-062606` as evidence; its takes were holed (F2), so the test is the evidence.)
13. **The swell's focus follows the finger with today's `GlassGlow` spring**, not re-timed: `GlassPress.followAnimation` is `GlassAnimation.dampedSpring(response: 1, dampingFraction: 1)`, the spring `GlassGlowLayerState` already moves its glow with (`Motion.smoothSpring(duration: Duration(seconds: 1))`). **It is the one press number not written by `pressfit`**: a value carried over, not a fit. If Task 15's drag scenes fail on the light during the drag, it is the first candidate.

*Frame cost (spec M10)*

14. **Animated glass costs 8 % more raster time than still glass, and a sliding layout 13 %, under the 20 % budget, and drops frames** (`R/perf-1.json`, four takes in alternating order, raster median of the take medians): `perf.material` 13.41 ms, `perf.motion` 14.50 ms (+8.1 %), `perf.slide` 15.16 ms (+13.0 %). **They produce 32 % and 44 % fewer frames** in their 6 s windows (979 and 808 against 1440 over four takes) and their raster p90 (17.37 and 18.72 ms) is over a 60 Hz frame: the median is spec M10's measure; the dropped frames are reported in Done item 4, not judged. `probe.perf` returns only the medians, so the prototype has no pairs; Task 15 keeps every take.

*Operator (spec §2, §8 item 6; D9, the user's decision)*

15. **Operator's pressable glass uses the package press** (Task 10, separable). `GlassSurface(pressable: true, enabled: true)` is `Glass.interactive()`; `_PressLift` (an `AnimatedScale` of 1.08 with a 0.35 or 0.25 white `GlassGlow`) is gone. Its tests keep and strengthen the behaviour: pressed and lit after down, grown after 0.5 s, released after up and after cancel, never pressed when disabled, released and at rest after a mid-press disable, pressed without growth under Reduce Motion (read through `debugGlassPress`, exported for tests). What changes for the user is in D9.

*How the prototype's numbers were taken*

16. **The take check (F1, R1, S1) looks at each touch's whole span, and judges lost frames everywhere except inside native's own 20 Hz cadence** (`takecheck.span_holes`, tests in `test_takecheck.py` and `test_press.py`). The span runs from a touch-down to the next touch, or to 1.2 s after the last touch-up. A take has a capture hole when:
   - **a squeezed first frame**: the first frame after a touch-down is under 4.5 ms after it (where clean takes read 5.0–6.7 ms; a squeezed rise reads 5–25 ms early, the re-review's table);
   - **a burst**: two consecutive frame gaps under 4.5 ms (3.3 ms or less on the recording's 1/600 s clock) anywhere in the span, the cut included; native's release cadence alternates single short and long gaps (13.3, 15.0, 16.7, 3.3, 11.7, 5.0 ms) and never has two short ones in a row, which is how it is told apart from a burst, and why 2B.1's excluded `dark-photo` take, whose release is that cadence, passes;
   - **frames lost while the glass moves**: a gap over 30 ms whose frame before differs from the frame before it, and whose frame after differs from the frame after it (the glass or its light moved on both sides: a mean absolute change over 0.2 at point scale inside the tracked region). For Flutter, anywhere in the span. **For native, from touch-down to 90 ms after it and from touch-up to the end of the span; in between, while native holds the press and draws its growth tail at 20 Hz, a gap counts only when it is longer than that cadence, 60 ms.** The evidence (`R/gap_phases.py`, `R/gap-phases.txt`, 56 native takes: the 24 N1 and N2 references, the 30 sampled 2B.1 noise takes, the 44 × 44 circle and the 250 × 150 rect): native's first gap over 30 ms comes 103.3 ms or more after a touch-down and 238.3 ms or more after a touch-up, and after a touch-up only with the glass nearly still (the light holding before its cut): no gap over 30 ms with the glass moving on both sides starts after any touch-up, so the test runs to the end of the span there (the second re-review's S1: 0 of 54 clean N1, N2 and noise takes flagged). Between the onset and touch-up, 87 of the 88 moving gaps over 30 ms are 33.3–56.7 ms long and start 103–265 ms after a touch-down, in 43 of the 56 takes: native's own 20 Hz tail of the growth, which the rule exempts up to 60 ms and no further (the other moving gap, 118.3 ms in a press-drag's hold, is a hole). **The onset window ends at 90 ms, not 100 (S5):** the earliest 20 Hz gap in a press take starts 103.3 ms after touch-down, but in `material.drag.far` `20261009-084517` one starts at 96 ms, so a 100 ms window had 3 ms of margin and flagged that take for its own cadence; at 90 ms the margin is 6 ms to the earliest seen and 13 ms to the press takes', and a 30–60 ms gap between 90 and 100 ms is the one loss this gives up. Task 12 reports how many takes the onset window flags; more than one in ten is a reason to stop and look. A gap of the 20 Hz cadence cannot be told from a lost frame by frame times, so two measures are protected only by the burst test and the leverage stop: `grow_ms` (it is read in the 20 Hz tail) and the cut's `glow_drop_ms`, `glow_fade_ms` and `glow_level` (a late cut frame after a still hold leaves no moving gap). `takecheck.check` applies these tests to a take (the `capture.<app>` gate, `run`'s re-record, `repeat`'s exclusion); `press.features` applies them again to each step's rows to mark that step's times NaN, with the native rule for both apps and with a glow change over 0.5 luma or a size or centre change over 0.5 pt as the motion: a still glass's rows move by up to 0.31 luma (Flutter after its cut, `20261009-123308`, 250 × 44 dark `photo`) and by single 1/3 pt tracker steps (native's hold before the cut, N1 `material.interactive` dark `stripes` step 3), which at the first round's 0.1 read as motion. **Neither of the third round's two changes loosens a judged limit.** (1) The rows' motion thresholds (0.5 luma, 0.5 pt) are not a limit that a measure is judged against: they only decide which steps `press.features` itself marks NaN, on top of the take-level verdict, which is unchanged (`takecheck.check` on the tracked region, motion over 0.2 at point scale; a take that fails it is re-recorded by `run`, excluded by `repeat` and fails the `capture.<app>` gate). At 0.1 the rows read a still glass's encoder and tracker jitter as motion and marked steps NaN that the region check passes (N1 `material.interactive` dark `stripes` step 3: a 98.3 ms gap in the still hold before the cut, glow moving 0.17–0.29 luma and the width one 1/3 pt tracker step); at 0.5, every rows hole found is also a region hole (`R/hole-rule-check.txt` against `R/native-press-all.txt`). **The converse does not hold: at 0.5 the rows do not see some step holes the take check finds** (2B.1's excluded `dark-stripes` step 1, and noise take 3's 118.3 ms hold loss in step 3; the third re-review's T4). So the take check, not the row thresholds, decides what a holed step counts: a take that fails it is re-recorded by `run` and excluded from floors and fits by `repeat` and `pressfit`, and in Task 15's Done counts a case whose `capture.<app>` gate fails counts every motion measure as failing. (2) The rule is a gap *over* 30 ms; the recordings' timestamps are on a 1/600 s clock, where 30.0 ms is 18 ticks and was computed as 0.030000000000001 s, so a gap of exactly 30.0 ms was flagged by floating-point error, not by the rule (Flutter `20261013-084127`, 300 × 250); gaps are now rounded to 0.1 ms before the comparison (test `test_a_gap_of_exactly_thirty_ms_on_the_recording_clock_is_not_over_the_limit`). One lost frame at 60 Hz is 33.3 ms (20 ticks) and still counts. Tests: `test_a_native_gap_while_the_glass_moves_late_in_the_release_is_lost` (100 ms at +300 ms after touch-up), `test_a_native_gap_longer_than_its_twenty_hertz_period_while_held_is_lost`, `test_the_archived_dark_stripes_release_is_a_hole_with_its_lost_frames_and_its_burst`, `test_native_s_slow_cadence_after_its_onset_is_not_lost_and_the_same_gaps_in_flutter_are`, `test_a_flickering_glow_after_the_cut_with_frames_far_apart_is_not_a_hole`.
   Applied to what exists (`R/hole-rule-check.txt`): N1 and N2, **20 of 24** clean (three squeezed first frames, and the 58 pt circle on dark `stripes` with a burst at its cut, 618 ms after touch-up, which the first round's 150 ms rule passed); 2B.1's excluded takes, `dark-photo` passes and `dark-stripes` fails (a burst 405 ms after touch-up and 101.7 ms lost 187 ms after it); the 30 sampled 2B.1 noise takes, **17 clean**: 12 squeezed first frames and take 3 of `material.interactive` dark `photo`, with 118.3 ms lost in its press-drag's hold, longer than native's 20 Hz cadence (the change from the second round's 18); the six Reduce Motion takes, 6 clean; the question takes, 6 of 8 (two squeezed; `material.drag.far` `20261009-084517`, flagged at 96 ms by the 100 ms window, is clean at 90 ms); the 250 × 150 and 300 × 250 takes in both apps, clean; the Flutter takes recorded after the second refit, 21 clean of the 24 cases kept (`20261013-*`: three squeezed or burst after three attempts each, and again after a reboot; they count as failing). The rate in the recordings after this rule's fresh boots is 40–55 % of the attempts (2026-10-13: 52 attempts, 27 holed, almost all squeezed first frames; 2026-10-09: 46 attempts, 19 holed; `R/STATUS.md`). So `run` re-records up to twice and Tasks 12, 13 and 15 budget for it. A holed step reads its time measures as NaN, which fails when judged and drops out of every floor; a take that fails the check is moved out of a floor by `repeat`.
17. **The press scenes are judged on `delay_ms` and the thirteen press measures** (Task 2); 2B.1 recorded `material.interactive` and `material.press.*` as references with no motion list.
18. **F20, for D1:** the Dart mirror of the union, scanned across a row of three 44 pt circles with the middle one at × 1.38 (a pressed 44 pt glass), at `spacing` 20 and 8 and gaps 8 and 16, is continuous (largest step under 1e-6, where the same scan finds the known cluster's step), test group in `angle_union_test.dart` (Task 9). A pressed glass in a row therefore does not reach the seam; D1 stays A.
19. **The new scenes run on `photo` only** (`material.drag.*`, `material.neighbours.*`, `material.press.circle44`, `.capsule36`): `stripes` adds gotcha 35's blind band to every size and position measure, and the light on `stripes` is judged on the six N2 sizes, which keep both backdrops. It halves the new scenes' recording (Task 13: 30 new cases, not 60).

*Carry-ins (ROADMAP §9 step 1, `todo-2b2.md`, `todo-2b1.md`), each ruled*

- **No single take sets a limit** (todo-2b2 audit A3, gotcha 52; D3, decided in its stronger form): `repeat` stops when one take sets a time limit above 3 × the median of the pairs without it (Task 3) and writes which pair sets every limit.

Task 15 re-runs `material.morph` once and reports whether `events.unpaired` changed now that its toggle presses.
- **Per-glass materials in one layer, the union's dark material, the shadow falloff, the N7 pinch** (todo-2b2): project 3.

**Decisions.** The user decided D6 and D9 on 2026-10-09 and D12 and D13 on 2026-10-14. The main session ruled D1, D3 and D5 in the review's fix round, and D11 in the second re-review's. The rest are the user's; each is a separable block or a deferral, and the plan runs whichever way they go. Recommendations first.
- **D1. The `angleSmoothUnion` seam:** **A, project 3**, ruled after F20's test (ruling 18) showed a pressed glass in a row keeps the field continuous.
- **D2. Morph's round-trip measure:** recommendation **A, a 2B fix wave**: a measure change unrelated to press. B: an excursion measure now (a prototype round, no recording).
- **D3. Single takes setting limits:** ruled: the leverage stop (Task 3, ruling 16).
- **D4. `FakeGlass` ignores visibility, motion and the press:** recommendation **A, project 3** (the no-shader path cannot be measured in the lab). B: one task with tests, not prototyped.
- **D5. Checks inside `repeat` and `run`:** ruled B, built (Task 3).
- **D6. Native's lean toward a finger outside the glass, an explicit departure from spec (a2): decided A by the user.** Native shows a lean toward the finger past the edge (stretch up to −0.035, centre 1.67 pt); spec (a2) says build where native shows it; the plan defers the build to a fix wave after Task 13's floors, and the user chose that deferral (A). Until then `material.drag.{out,up,far}` fail `stretch` and `centre`, classed (a). Option B, building it in 2B.3, is kept as Task 14b only as a record: **it is marked not to run**, was never prototyped, and no other task depends on it.
- **D7. The other 2B.2 class (a) items:** recommendation **A, a 2B fix wave**. B: here, with a prototype round and about 2 h of recording.
- **D8. todo-2b1's package defects:** recommendation **A, the same fix wave**. B: one task with a test each.
- **D9. Operator's press swap (Task 10): decided A by the user on 2026-10-09**, kept in 2B.3 on condition of the 44 × 44 native reference (Task 12, held out in Task 14) and the behavioural tests (ruling 15), shown to the user before merge, and separable (no other task depends on it). What it changes: Operator's 44 pt icon buttons and bar items now grow by the law's 15.84 pt, × 1.36 (with the first round's law it was × 1.38) where `_PressLift` scaled × 1.08; their light is the 58 pt circle's (the table's end), a hold of 9.8 luma and a swell of 32.6 luma, a white flash in place of a 0.35 white `GlassGlow`, holding about 0.65 s after release before it goes in one frame; under Reduce Motion they now light, where `_PressLift` did nothing; the terminal chat header is one interactive capsule around several controls, so pressing any of them grows the whole capsule; and the corners of a compact button's 44 pt hit area outside its 36 pt capsule tap without lighting. **The 44 × 44 result** (held out of the fit): one clean native take (`20261009-103129`, dark `photo`) against one Flutter take recorded with the refitted table, judged at fixed thresholds, 10 of 15 pass (`R/heldout.txt`, Flutter `20261014-165002`). The size passes: native grows 16.67 × 17.33 pt, Flutter 17.0 × 17.33 (the law's cap of 15.84 pt settled, with the spring's overshoot on top), so the law extrapolates to 44 pt within 0.33 pt. The light does not: native's peak is 53.5 luma and Flutter's 43.2 (10.3 under), native cuts 750 ms after release where Flutter, on the table's end, cuts at 666.7, its fade's half is 27 ms late, and the release is 85 ms faster in Flutter (`shrink_ms` 250 against 335). So at Operator's main size the growth is native's and the light is dimmer and shorter than native's, by the amounts above; the light per size table holds its smallest measured size (58 pt) below it.
- **D10. Native's light is not white; the package's is** (ruling 6): native's light halves in luma over light `stripes` (solid) and seems to change colour there (suggestive only: 4:2:0 chroma). Recommendation **A, a fix wave** that drives the glass material's brightness and saturation with the press (the `LiquidGlassSettings` the shader already has), the model held-out RMS chose (6.14 against white's 9.51 and screen's 12.45), fitted by a tool on the four N1 cases; until then light `stripes` fails `glow_peak`, class (b). B: prototype it now (a shader-side model, about one prototype round).
- **D11. The law's form for tall glass: ruled by the main session (re-review 2, S2)**: the form must keep every measured height within the 1 pt size threshold before leave-one-size-out chooses (ruling 8); the 250 × 150 rect joins the fit and a new tall size, 300 × 250, is held out.
- **D12. Growth in width and height: decided A by the user**, done in this plan (ruling 8): the law is fitted as a function of width and height over every measured size, with a new held-out portrait size.
- **D13. Portrait glass: decided A by the user on 2026-10-14.** The plan ships the current law, min(15.84, 9.73 × (100 / h)^1.24), and lets execution decide with a stop rule: Task 14 refits the law from Tasks 12 and 13's fresh data (about 12 sizes, the held-out ones apart) with the same selection rule (every measured size within the 1 pt threshold first, then leave-one-size-out); **if no form passes at every measured size, Task 14 stops**, writes nothing to `ios27_press.dart` (`pressfit` refuses; test `test_a_refit_where_no_form_keeps_every_size_within_the_threshold_writes_nothing`) and reports to the main session with the per-size residuals. The known misses, the 120 × 250 portrait (−1.55 pt) and 300 × 250 (−0.88 pt), are declared in the forecast and the README as measured limits of the shipped law.

## Global Constraints

- No code comments in any new or changed code (Dart, Swift, Python, GLSL, shell); keep the upstream comments that already exist.
- Every commit message ends with the `Co-Authored-By:` line the executing session is given.
- Never stage `frontend/package-lock.json`. Never `git stash`. Never run `dart format` on whole files; match the surrounding style.
- Build the lab apps only with `python3 tool/glass_lab/harness/lab.py build <native|example|operator|all>` (plain `flutter build ios` fails under Xcode 27).
- Use only the simulator "iPhone 17 Pro (iOS 27)", UDID `708879DD-8B2A-4547-863F-F49EE1474D8B`, and name it in every `simctl` command, never `booted`; never touch the iOS 26.5 simulator (`94D0C207-A90B-4806-BBAB-8AF9B3F16329`) or its Operator data.
- Simulator or system prompts get only "Don't Allow" or "Not Now"; never pair Operator; never enter a password.
- No downloads, no `pip install`, no new dependencies; run `flutter test` and `flutter analyze` with `--no-pub` (the workspace resolves offline).
- Never push. Never merge.
- Limits and measures are never loosened: the fixed thresholds in `metrics.py` stay (this plan adds `ratio` 0.01, spec L4's), every limit is max(fixed, 1.5 × noise).
- Fitted tables are written only by tools: `ios27.dart` and `ios27_scroll_edge.dart` by `lab.py tune --write`, `ios27_motion.dart` by `lab.py fitvis --write`, `ios27_press.dart` by `pressfit.py fit --write` (Task 5's patch carries the prototype's output and Task 5 checks that the tool writes it byte for byte; Task 14 rewrites it from the executor's takes). **No fit result is reported as a pass on the takes it was fitted on**: Task 14's refit uses the reference and noise takes, and Task 15's Done runs record native again, so each Done run's native side is a recording the fit has not seen; the results say which measures were also fitted (the size law, the springs, the cut per size, the light per size) and that those passes are on new recordings of the same scenes, not on new scenes; the 44 × 44 circle and the 120 × 250 portrait capsule are the held-out sizes.
- When a patch does not apply, or a run's output differs from its "Expected output" block or its stated outcome, the executor does not improvise: it finds the cause, says in the task report what differs and why, and **stops and asks the main session** if the cause is not a path or a number that depends on the machine.
- Flutter 3.44.5.
- One lab command at a time (`build`, `run`, `repeat`, `fitvis`, `tune`, `perf`, `a11y`, `reboot` share the simulator); run long ones with the Bash tool's `run_in_background`, never a trailing `&`, and never poll with short sleeps. **No analysis, test run or build on the Mac while a native take records or `perf` runs**: the prototype's own analysis squeezed a 1.0 s press to 0.607 s (ruling 16), and raster times read the Mac's load. Stop a lab run only with SIGINT (gotcha 50); an orphaned `recordVideo` is stopped with SIGINT too.
- Reboot the simulator (`lab.py reboot`) before recording native references, **every two hours of recording, and whenever three attempts in a row fail the take check**: in the prototype the share of holed takes rose from about half to 14 of 22 attempts about two hours after a boot, with a press squeezed to 0.132 s (`R/STATUS.md`, run `20261009-101613`; gotcha 49). After every boot, a native `material.interactive` take must pass the take check (its press reads within 20 % of 1.0 s).
- After any interrupted lab command, `xcrun simctl spawn 708879DD-8B2A-4547-863F-F49EE1474D8B defaults read com.apple.Accessibility ReduceMotionEnabled` and `… EnhancedBackgroundContrastEnabled` must print `0`; if not, run `python3 -c "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'tool/glass_lab/harness'); import sim; sim.accessibility(sim.device(), 'none')"`.
- **Every press take passes the take check** (ruling 16): `run` re-records a failing one up to twice and `repeat` keeps failing ones out of the floors; a kept take that still fails is recorded again on its own after a reboot. No floor, timing fit or reference uses a holed take; the size law alone reads every take's plateau, holed or not, because a hole moves times and the plateau is read 0.3 s before touch-up (ruling 8).
- `df -h /Users/omaraly` before the archive copy (Task 13), before every group of Tasks 12 and 13, before every run of Task 15; analysis writes frame caches into the recordings (gotcha 51). **Stop under 40 GB free and report.** Record the free space measured at the start of execution in the Task 0 report (about 55 GB were free on 2026-10-14; the user may free more before execution). The only thing a task may delete to make room is the analysis frame caches (`overview/`, `shapes/`, `marker/`) of takes already analysed in this execution's own run folders (Task 13 Step 1b); never a recording, never anything in the archives or the prototype's runs; anything else needs the main session's yes.
- A case whose `driver.log` holds only the `xcodebuild` invocation line is a known driver crash: rerun that case alone with `--appearance` and `--backdrop`, and say so.
- Judge glass only by lab measurements; when a number looks wrong, open the frames. Record every number with its run folder.
- `lab.py repeat` and `lab.py run` stop with `the native app build is older than its sources` (or the example's) when a source changed since the last build: run `python3 tool/glass_lab/harness/lab.py build all` and repeat the command. Editing a package or example file while a run records makes the rest of that run refuse (the prototype lost two runs to it).
- Paths are relative to `packages/mobile/` unless they start with `docs/` or `packages/mobile/`. Run every patch from the worktree root as `git apply --3way`.

## Execution setup

- First, the main session commits this plan and `docs/liquid_glass/02b-motion/research/proto-2b3/` to `development` (they live only on `proto/2b3` until then).
- Then work in a new worktree on a new branch from `development`:
  `git -C /Users/omaraly/development/AI/Operator worktree add /Users/omaraly/development/AI/Operator-2b3 -b feat/ios-liquid-glass-2b3 development`
  and run `cd packages/mobile && flutter pub get --offline` (Task 0).
- This runs only on this Mac: a cloud session cannot reach the simulator.
- Never touch `/Users/omaraly/development/AI/Operator` (the shared checkout) after the `worktree add`.
- Run the lab from `packages/mobile`. Pass run folders to `--into` as absolute paths (gotcha 43). Install the backdrops after the first build with
  `python3 -c "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'tool/glass_lab/harness'); import sim, build; u = sim.device(); sim.status_bar(u); [sim.install_backdrops(u, b, build.backdrops()) for b in (build.NATIVE_BUNDLE, build.EXAMPLE_BUNDLE)]"`.
- Recordings are copied, never moved, from `/Users/omaraly/development/AI/glass-lab-runs/2b1/` (the archive is only read): `runs/noise-2b1` (Task 13), `runs/20261006-085522`, `-085825` and `-191235` (N1 and N2, Task 14). The prototype's runs (`/Users/omaraly/development/AI/Operator-2b3-proto/packages/mobile/build/glass_lab/runs/20261009-*`) are read in place and never written.
- From Task 12 on, shell snippets assume `cd /Users/omaraly/development/AI/Operator-2b3/packages/mobile`, `R=/Users/omaraly/development/AI/Operator-2b3/docs/liquid_glass/02b-motion/research/proto-2b3` and `R2=/Users/omaraly/development/AI/Operator-2b3/docs/liquid_glass/02b-motion/research/proto-2b2`.
- Gates for every task that touches their code:
  - app, from `packages/mobile`: `flutter analyze --no-pub` prints "No issues found!", `flutter test --no-pub` is green;
  - package, from `packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass`: `flutter analyze --no-pub`, `flutter test --no-pub`;
  - example, from `packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/example`: `flutter analyze --no-pub`, `flutter test --no-pub`;
  - harness, from `packages/mobile`: `python3 -m unittest discover tool/glass_lab/harness/tests` prints OK;
  - `flutter test` prints a long SkSL error about `liquid_glass_geometry_blended` ("initializers are not permitted on arrays"); it is known and harmless (ROADMAP gotcha 3).

## Review Focus

1. **The press measures judge absence as well as presence, and a holed step is never a pass.** A glass that lights or grows in one app and not the other fails its time measures (inf); both quiet agree (0); a step with a capture hole reads its times as NaN, which fails and drops out of every floor. Pinned by `test_absent_times_on_both_sides_agree_and_on_one_side_fail`, `test_a_burst_after_touch_down_marks_the_step_and_its_times_are_judged_as_absent` and `test_a_press_measure_with_no_touch_window_is_absent_and_fails` (Task 1).
2. **The take rule** (ruling 16): over the whole span from touch-down to the next touch, a squeezed first frame, a burst of two short gaps (the cut included), or frames lost while the glass moves (for native, inside the windows where it always draws at 60 Hz) is a hole; native's alternating release cadence and its slow cadence after the onset are not. Pinned by `test_a_burst_anywhere_from_touch_down_to_the_next_touch_is_a_capture_hole` (a burst at +450 ms, the cut), `test_the_archived_dark_stripes_release_is_a_hole_with_its_lost_frames_and_its_burst` (2B.1's excluded take's gap list), `test_native_s_slow_cadence_after_its_onset_is_not_lost_and_the_same_gaps_in_flutter_are`, `test_a_gap_while_the_glass_moves_in_native_s_onset_is_lost`, `test_a_gap_after_a_still_frame_or_with_no_frame_after_it_in_the_span_is_not_lost`, `test_the_release_cadence_of_alternating_short_and_long_gaps_is_not_a_hole` and `test_a_first_frame_squeezed_onto_the_touch_down_frame_is_a_hole` (`test_takecheck.py`, Task 3, on Task 1's `takecheck.py`); `run`'s re-record and `repeat`'s exclusion and leverage stop by `CheckedRecordingTests`, `LeverageStopTests` and `LeverageTests`.
3. **Glow first, growth second, one uniform scale on the settled law in height and width, the cut per size.** Pinned by `a press lights the glass at once and grows it after the growth delay, uniformly, to the size law` and `on release the glow stays until the cut and drops to nothing in one frame` (Task 5), and in `pressfit` by `test_the_law_is_fitted_on_the_settled_growth_so_a_spring_overshoot_is_not_counted_twice`, `test_the_law_reads_every_take_s_plateau_holed_or_not`, `test_a_law_in_width_and_height_is_recovered_on_portrait_and_landscape_sizes`, `test_a_form_that_misses_a_measured_size_by_more_than_the_size_threshold_is_not_chosen`, `test_a_refit_where_no_form_keeps_every_size_within_the_threshold_writes_nothing`, `test_the_glow_profile_is_the_median_of_its_takes` and `test_the_cut_is_read_per_size_and_a_size_without_one_takes_its_nearest` (Task 4).
4. **Touches and lifecycle.** The eleven touch and lifecycle tests of `glass_press_test.dart` (ruling 11) and Operator's restored behaviour tests (ruling 15).
5. **Tool-written numbers, and exactly what `pressfit` guards.** `ios27_press.dart` equals `pressfit.py fit --write`'s output (Task 5's check) and is rewritten only by the tool (Task 14). `problems()` refuses to write when: no growth law form keeps every measured size within the 1 pt size threshold (test `test_a_refit_where_no_form_keeps_every_size_within_the_threshold_writes_nothing`: the table is left untouched); the chosen law is on its grid edge (cap 14–20 pt, growth at 100 pt 4–40 pt, height power 0.5–2, width power −1 to 1); sigma is on its grid edge (20–300 pt); more than 25 % of the per-take spring fits (growth, release, light rise, light fade) sit on their grid edge (response 0.01–1.5 s, damping 0.1–4); the Reduce Motion cut rests on fewer than five presses; any of the growth, release, rise, hold, fade, cut or Reduce Motion cut fits is missing; or there is no size table. **Not guarded:** the hold, swell and cut per size are medians with no minimum count (one clean take sets a size's row; `cases` in `pressfit.json` says how many), and among the forms that keep every size within 1 pt the lower leave-one-size-out RMS chooses, even when two forms are not separated (`law.separated` says so; ruling 8). Pinned by `test_problems_name_spring_fits_on_the_grid_edge_and_a_thin_reduce_motion_cut` and `test_write_refuses_a_summary_with_problems_and_writes_one_without`.
6. **What is not built and why**: no glow spreading and no container glow layer (ruling 4), no release while the finger is down (ruling 3), no lean toward a far finger (the user's D6 A; Task 14b is not to run), a white light (D10). A reviewer checks that no code path draws light on a neighbour or deforms the glass on a drag.
7. **Not pinned, said:** the light's look (the plus-mode paint cannot be read in `flutter test`, which draws `FakeGlass`, gotcha 39); the press-drag cut's cause (ruling 7); portrait glass growth (ruling 8); the light between and beyond the six sizes (ruling 6); the scroll takeover against native (ruling 11); whether the light follows the finger under Reduce Motion (ruling 10).

## What this plan expects to reach

Measured in the prototype at the **fixed thresholds only** (no press floor exists yet; a floor can only raise a limit). Flutter was recorded on 2026-10-14 with the package as this plan ships it (the two-variable refit), on every case of the six 2B.1 sizes and on 250 × 150 and 300 × 250 (runs `20261014-165348`, `-165947`, `-170318`, `-170941`, `-171353`, `-171807`, `-165054`, `-165221`); in 4 of the 26 cases the kept Flutter take still fails the take check after three attempts (all on `stripes`), and in 4 the native reference does, so those times are NaN and count as failing. **Two columns (R2).** *In-sample*: against the archive's N1 and N2 references and the 250 × 150 and 300 × 250 takes (`R/compare_archived.py`, `R/flutter-compare.txt`), which `pressfit` fitted on, so their passes are not a forecast for recordings the fit has not seen. *Independent*: against the 17 sampled 2B.1 noise takes that pass the take check, which no fit read (`R/flutter-compare-independent.txt`). **The independent column is narrow (S6):** it covers three sizes (250 × 88, 250 × 44 and the 58 pt circle), on `photo` only, paired with six Flutter takes; 138 × 53, 300 × 120, 360 × 200, 250 × 150, 300 × 250 and every `stripes` case have in-sample numbers only. Counts are passing / judged (finite) / expected over the press measures and `delay_ms`; the event, touch and capture gates apart.

| Done item (spec §8, 2B.3) | Prototype | Forecast after Task 15 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 `material.interactive` and the N2 sizes: scale on the size law, glow first and growth second with native's timings, release, cut, delay | **In-sample: 318 of 469 measures pass over 26 cases (417 finite).** **Independent: 246 of 347 over 17 pairs (every measure finite), 71 %**; on the same six cases the in-sample count is 92 of 124, 74 %. Independent, per case: `material.interactive` dark `photo` 48 / 62 and light `photo` 66 / 93, 250 × 44 46 / 64 and 38 / 48, the 58 pt circle 22 / 32 and 26 / 48. Independent failures by measure: `glow_half_ms` 18, `shrink_ms` 18, `delay_ms` 15, `glow_rise_ms` 11, `glow_drop_ms` 11, `glow_fade_ms` 11, `grow_ms` 9, `glow_peak` 5, `glow_drop` 2, `glow_level` 1. Gates: `events.unpaired` fails in 6 in-sample cases; `capture.native` in 4, `capture.flutter` in 4. Static `bbox_pt`, `centre_pt`, `rim_rms` or `luminance` fail at rest in 2A and 2B.1's residual cases (the still check of Task 15 Step 5 compares them with `development`). **`shrink_ms`, the largest failure, classed (a) with evidence (S3, `R/shrink_check.py`, `R/shrink-check.txt`):** native's release time depends on the size, as its cut does: medians 323–335 ms for the 58 and 44 pt circles, 278 for 138 × 53, 255 for 250 × 88, 152 for 250 × 44, 148 for 250 × 150 and 132 for 300 × 250 (the 58 pt circle's eight clean takes within 315–340 ms); the package releases every size on one spring, and its medians, 167–300 ms, are not ordered by size. The 2/3 pt band is not the cause: read with 10 % of the growth alone the times are the same for every growth over 6.7 pt, and differ only at 250 × 150 and 300 × 250, where the growth is small. The candidate is a release spring per size, fitted as the cut is (todo). Held out (ruling 8): 44 × 44 10 of 15 (growth passes), 120 × 250 portrait 6 of 16 (growth fails). **Measured limits of the shipped law (D13):** the 120 × 250 portrait −1.55 pt and 300 × 250 −0.88 pt; Task 14 stops if its refit finds no form within 1 pt at every size | **Failing in part.** Floors will raise the time limits: the probe on 250 × 44 (`R/noise-recompute-probe.txt`, first round) gave native press noises of 13–40 ms, so limits of 20–60 ms, which several of today's time failures sit under. Expected to stay failing, classed (a) or (b): `shrink_ms` where native's release depends on size (a, above), the cut after a press-drag (ruling 7), portrait growth (D13, a measured limit of the shipped law), light on light `stripes` (ruling 6, D10), the 58 pt and 44 pt circles' light, `events.unpaired`, and `delay_ms` where Flutter answers in the frame of the touch while native's onset lags (2B.1 ruling 33: reported, not copied). Task 15's Done runs record native again, so their counts are of the independent kind. |

Flutter not run on these scenes in the prototype | **Failing in part:** `material.drag.{out,up,far}` fail `stretch` and `centre` (the lean is not built, D6); the neighbours pass on absence; the pressed glass's size measures at gaps 0 and 10 fail as merge-model failures (ruling 4). |

Flutter not run under Reduce Motion | **Failing in part:** the press passes on growth (none on both) and is judged on the light and cut; materialize under Reduce Motion carries 2B.1's 133 of 168 and 2B.2's 149 of 168, merge and morph under Reduce Motion carry 2B.2's failures (`todo-2b2.md`); 2B.3 changes none of them. |

Still check: `material.tinted` (interactive Run button) against 2B.2's run `20261008-232144`, `missing: 0`, `worse: 0`, all Flutter frames byte-identical (`R/still-check-tinted.txt`); `material.interactive` against the prototype's recording of `development` (`development` run `Operator-2b3-dev/.../20261009-101012` against the prototype's `20261009-101613`: `missing: 0`, `worse: 0`, every Flutter frame byte-identical, `R/still-check-interactive.txt`) | `missing: 0`, `worse: 0` in all ten scenes. |

**Time.** Tasks 0–11 (patches and gates) about 1.5 h; Task 12 about 1.5 h (45 min of recording with re-records); Task 13 about 8 h (5 h of recording over two sessions, 3 h of analysis, 8.6 GB copied); Task 14 about 1 h; Task 15 about 4 h (about 2.5 h of recording: sixteen scenes in both apps, three under Reduce Motion, the ten still scenes, the perf pairs and the A9 A/B); Task 16 about 1 h. **About 17 h in all, about 8 h of it on the simulator**, most of the growth over the first round's estimate being the take check's re-recordings (about 40 % of today's first attempts, ruling 16).

**The plan forecasts that Done items 1, 2 and 3 fail in part**, and the event-pairing gate with them, each failure classed; merging is then the user's decision on classed failures, as spec §8 allows.

## Tasks

Tasks 1–11 are the prototype's code, each split into a test patch and an implementation patch where it has tests, so every new test is seen to fail first (Task 9 adds tests that pass on arrival, by design); Task 10 is separable (D9). Tasks 12–16 are recordings, fits and verification that only execution can do (12 the native references, 13 the noise floors, 14 the refit, 15 the Done runs and frame cost, 16 the results and documents).

### Task 0: Setup

- [ ] **Step 1: Resolve the workspace offline.**

RUN[t00-setup]: `cd packages/mobile && flutter pub get --offline` => PASS

Expected output ends with:

```text
62 packages have newer versions incompatible with dependency constraints.
Try `flutter pub outdated` for more information.
```

### Task 1: L4: press measures per touch step, and the capture-hole rule that keeps squeezed frames out of them

**Files:**
- Create: `tool/glass_lab/harness/press.py`, `tool/glass_lab/harness/takecheck.py` (the take rule: touch count, scripted durations, capture holes)
- Modify: `tool/glass_lab/harness/shapes.py` (a `glow` row over the rest box or a pinned rect, `press.*` in `MOTION_MEASURES`, `compare`, `measures`, `expected`, `limits`), `track.py` (`glow`), `metrics.py` (threshold `ratio` 0.01), `manifest.py` (`press.*`, `pinned`), `reproduce.py` (`glow`)
- Test: `tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_press.py`

**Why.** Rulings 1, 2 and 16. Spec L4 is built first so the lab measures glow and stretch before any package press work. A step with a capture hole anywhere from its touch-down to the next touch (a squeezed first frame, a burst of two short gaps, or frames lost while the glass moves, ruling 16) reads its time measures as NaN: they fail when judged and drop out of every floor (F1, R1).

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the failing tests.**

Patch `t01-tests` (`c0e69d9a4..87b88ab02`, 1 files):

```diff
diff --git a/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_press.py b/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_press.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b9f2a8ae8ba95ae6b1920c4bf26ecf626862e1c1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_press.py
@@ -0,0 +1,226 @@
+import math
+import sys
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
+
+import manifest
+import metrics
+import press
+import shapes
+
+
+def row(glow=100.0, width=250.0, height=88.0, cx=201.0, cy=451.0):
+    return {"glow": glow, "width": width, "height": height, "cx": cx, "cy": cy}
+
+
+def native_press(drop_at=0.400, rise_frames=(13.0, 18.0, 20.0, 20.6), grow=12.0, up=1.0, step=1 / 60):
+    times, rows = [0.0], [row()]
+    t = 0.5
+    for value in rise_frames:
+        t += step
+        times.append(t)
+        rows.append(row(glow=100 + value))
+    while t < up:
+        t += step
+        times.append(t)
+        share = min(1.0, (t - 0.5) / 0.2)
+        rows.append(row(glow=120.6, width=250 + grow * share, height=88 + grow * share * 88 / 250))
+    released = t
+    while t < released + 1.0:
+        t += step
+        times.append(t)
+        after = t - up
+        glow = 100.0 if after >= drop_at else 120.6 - 8.6 * after / drop_at
+        share = max(0.0, 1 - after / 0.15)
+        rows.append(row(glow=glow, width=250 + grow * share, height=88 + grow * share * 88 / 250))
+    return times, rows
+
+
+class PressFeatureTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_glow_first_growth_second_and_a_one_frame_drop_after_release(self):
+        times, rows = native_press()
+        found = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["glow_peak"], 20.6, places=6)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["glow_rise_ms"], 3 * 1000 / 60, places=3)
+        self.assertGreater(found["grow_ms"], found["glow_rise_ms"])
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["grow_w"], 12.0, places=6)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["glow_drop"], 12.0, delta=0.5)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["glow_drop_ms"], 400, delta=1000 / 60)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["glow_fade_ms"], found["glow_drop_ms"], delta=1e-6)
+        self.assertLess(found["shrink_ms"], 170)
+        self.assertLess(abs(found["stretch"]), 0.01)
+        self.assertEqual(found["centre"], 0.0)
+
+    def test_a_glow_that_decays_smoothly_has_no_drop_time(self):
+        times, rows = native_press(drop_at=10.0)
+        found = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertLess(found["glow_drop"], press.MIN_GLOW)
+        self.assertIsNone(found["glow_drop_ms"])
+
+    def test_the_rest_is_the_last_frame_before_touch_down(self):
+        times = [0.0, 0.5, 0.52, 0.6]
+        rows = [row(glow=100), row(glow=130), row(glow=130), row(glow=130)]
+        found = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 0.55))
+        self.assertEqual(found["rest"]["glow"], 100)
+        self.assertEqual(found["glow_peak"], 30)
+        self.assertEqual(found["glow_rise_ms"], 0)
+
+    def test_a_shape_that_does_not_light_has_no_times(self):
+        times = [0.0, 0.6, 0.7, 1.2]
+        rows = [row(), row(glow=101), row(glow=101.5), row()]
+        found = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertLess(found["glow_peak"], press.MIN_GLOW)
+        for key in ("glow_rise_ms", "glow_fade_ms", "glow_drop_ms", "grow_ms", "shrink_ms"):
+            self.assertIsNone(found[key])
+        self.assertEqual(found["glow_drop"], 0.0)
+
+    def test_stretch_is_the_signed_ratio_change_and_centre_the_largest_offset(self):
+        times = [0.0, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0, 1.4]
+        rows = [row(), row(width=260, height=88), row(width=250, height=80, cx=205, cy=454), row(), row()]
+        found = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["stretch"], (250 / 80) / (250 / 88) - 1, places=9)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["centre"], 5.0, places=9)
+
+    def test_the_release_stops_at_the_next_touch(self):
+        times, rows = native_press()
+        later = [t + 3.0 for t in times[1:]]
+        found = press.features(times + later, rows + rows[1:], (0.5, 1.0), end=3.5)
+        alone = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertEqual(found["glow_fade_ms"], alone["glow_fade_ms"])
+
+
+class PressHoleTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_a_burst_after_touch_down_marks_the_step_and_its_times_are_judged_as_absent(self):
+        times, rows = native_press()
+        burst = [0.0, 0.5, 0.5017, 0.5034, 0.5051] + [t + 0.01 for t in times[5:]]
+        found = press.features(burst, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertTrue(found["hole"])
+        clean = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertFalse(clean["hole"])
+        entry = press.compare(clean, found)
+        for measure in press.TIMES:
+            self.assertTrue(math.isnan(entry[measure]), measure)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(entry["glow_peak"], 0.0, places=6)
+
+    def test_a_burst_at_the_cut_marks_the_step_and_a_still_frame_far_apart_does_not(self):
+        times, rows = native_press()
+        cut = next(i for i, t in enumerate(times) if t > 1.45)
+        squeezed = list(times)
+        squeezed[cut + 1] = squeezed[cut] + 0.002
+        squeezed[cut + 2] = squeezed[cut] + 0.004
+        self.assertTrue(press.features(squeezed, rows, (0.5, 1.0))["hole"])
+        still = [0.0, 0.5, 0.9, 1.0, 1.2]
+        quiet = [row(), row(), row(), row(), row()]
+        self.assertFalse(press.features(still, quiet, (0.5, 1.0))["hole"])
+
+
+    def test_a_flickering_glow_after_the_cut_with_frames_far_apart_is_not_a_hole(self):
+        times, rows = native_press()
+        kept = [i for i, t in enumerate(times) if t < 1.5]
+        times = [times[i] for i in kept] + [1.65, 1.78, 1.79]
+        rows = [rows[i] for i in kept] + [row(glow=100.3), row(glow=100.0), row(glow=100.3)]
+        self.assertFalse(press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))["hole"])
+        lost = [t if t < 1.05 else t + 0.04 for t in native_press()[0]]
+        self.assertTrue(press.features(lost, native_press()[1], (0.5, 1.0))["hole"])
+
+
+class PressFadeTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_the_level_before_the_cut_and_the_half_time_judge_the_fade(self):
+        times, rows = native_press()
+        found = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["glow_level"], 12.0, delta=0.5)
+        self.assertGreater(found["glow_half_ms"], 0)
+        self.assertLess(found["glow_half_ms"], found["glow_drop_ms"])
+        slower = press.features(*native_press(drop_at=0.6), (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertGreater(slower["glow_half_ms"], found["glow_half_ms"])
+
+    def test_a_glow_without_a_cut_has_no_level_and_halves_toward_rest(self):
+        found = press.features(*native_press(drop_at=10.0), (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertIsNone(found["glow_level"])
+        self.assertIsNone(found["glow_half_ms"])
+        times = [0.0, 0.6, 0.9] + [1.0 + 0.02 * k for k in range(1, 11)]
+        rows = [row(), row(glow=120), row(glow=120)] + [row(glow=120 - 2 * k) for k in range(1, 11)]
+        faded = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertIsNone(faded["glow_level"])
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(faded["glow_half_ms"], 100.0, places=6)
+
+
+class PressCompareTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_absent_times_on_both_sides_agree_and_on_one_side_fail(self):
+        times, rows = native_press()
+        lit = press.features(times, rows, (0.5, 1.0))
+        steps = [0.0] + [0.5 + k / 60 for k in range(91)]
+        dark = press.features(steps, [row(glow=101 if 0.5 < t <= 1.0 else 100) for t in steps], (0.5, 1.0))
+        self.assertEqual(press.compare(dark, dark)["glow_rise_ms"], 0.0)
+        self.assertEqual(press.compare(lit, dark)["glow_rise_ms"], math.inf)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(press.compare(lit, dark)["glow_peak"], lit["glow_peak"] - dark["glow_peak"], places=9)
+
+    def test_every_press_measure_has_a_threshold(self):
+        for measure in press.MEASURES:
+            self.assertIn(press.LIMITS[measure], metrics.THRESHOLDS)
+        self.assertEqual(metrics.THRESHOLDS["ratio"], 0.01)
+        self.assertEqual(metrics.THRESHOLDS["luminance"], 3.0)
+        self.assertEqual(metrics.THRESHOLDS["centre_pt"], 1.0)
+        self.assertEqual(metrics.THRESHOLDS["time_ms"], 17.0)
+
+
+class PressSceneTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def scene(self, motion):
+        return manifest.parse([{
+            "id": "test.neighbours",
+            "group": "material",
+            "title": "two pressable glasses",
+            "inventory": "2.5",
+            "app": "lab",
+            "backdrops": ["photo"],
+            "appearances": ["dark"],
+            "steps": [{"wait": 0.5}, {"press": {"at": "left", "duration": 1.0}}, {"wait": 1.0}],
+            "regions": {"left": [60, 393, 141, 116], "right": [201, 393, 141, 116]},
+            "track": ["left", "right"],
+            "motion": list(motion),
+        }])[0]
+
+    def capture(self, glow):
+        times, rows = native_press()
+        rows = [dict(r, glow=100 + (r["glow"] - 100) * glow) for r in rows]
+        quiet = [row() for _ in rows]
+        return {"touches": [(0.5, 1.0)], "rows": {"left": {"times": times, "rows": rows}, "right": {"times": times, "rows": quiet}}, "events": []}
+
+    def test_every_touch_step_and_tracked_shape_is_expected_and_judged(self):
+        scene = self.scene(["press.glow_peak", "press.glow_rise_ms"])
+        result = shapes.compare(scene, self.capture(1.0), self.capture(0.5))
+        expected = shapes.expected(result, scene)
+        self.assertEqual(sorted(expected), sorted(f"{n}.step1.press.{m}" for n in ("left", "right") for m in ("glow_peak", "glow_rise_ms")))
+        limits = shapes.limits(result, scene)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(limits["left.step1.press.glow_peak"][0], 10.3, places=6)
+        self.assertEqual(limits["left.step1.press.glow_peak"][1], 3.0)
+        self.assertEqual(limits["right.step1.press.glow_peak"][0], 0.0)
+        self.assertEqual(limits["right.step1.press.glow_rise_ms"][0], 0.0)
+
+    def test_a_press_measure_with_no_touch_window_is_absent_and_fails(self):
+        scene = self.scene(["press.glow_peak"])
+        quiet = dict(self.capture(1.0), touches=[])
+        limits = shapes.limits(shapes.compare(scene, quiet, quiet), scene)
+        self.assertEqual(limits["left.step1.press.glow_peak"][0], math.inf)
+
+    def test_a_pinned_rect_must_lie_inside_its_tracked_region(self):
+        base = {
+            "id": "test.pinned", "group": "material", "title": "t", "inventory": "2.5", "app": "lab",
+            "backdrops": ["photo"], "appearances": ["dark"], "steps": [{"wait": 0.5}, {"press": {"at": "left", "duration": 1.0}}],
+            "regions": {"left": [60, 393, 141, 116]}, "track": ["left"],
+        }
+        self.assertEqual(manifest.validate([dict(base, pinned={"left": [80, 407, 60, 60]})]), [])
+        self.assertTrue(manifest.validate([dict(base, pinned={"left": [10, 407, 60, 60]})]))
+        self.assertTrue(manifest.validate([dict(base, pinned={"right": [80, 407, 60, 60]})]))
+
+    def test_a_pinned_rect_is_the_glow_box_in_region_pixels(self):
+        self.assertEqual(shapes.pinned_px([80, 407, 60, 60], (180, 1178, 423, 348)), (60, 42, 239, 223))
+
+    def test_the_manifest_and_the_analysis_list_the_same_press_measures(self):
+        self.assertEqual([m for m in manifest.MOTION_MEASURES if m.startswith("press.")], [f"press.{m}" for m in press.MEASURES])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()
```

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..46021175d7a91b5ebd8c0595af8e0a236055a667
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/press.py
@@ -0,0 +1,183 @@
+import math
+
+import numpy as np
+
+import takecheck
+import touch
+
+PEAK_SHARE = 0.9
+SETTLE_SHARE = 0.1
+MIN_GLOW = 3.0
+MIN_GROWTH = 1.0
+SIZE_BAND = 2.0 / 3.0
+MEASURES = (
+    "glow_peak",
+    "glow_rise_ms",
+    "glow_fade_ms",
+    "glow_drop",
+    "glow_drop_ms",
+    "glow_level",
+    "glow_half_ms",
+    "grow_w",
+    "grow_h",
+    "grow_ms",
+    "shrink_ms",
+    "stretch",
+    "centre",
+)
+TIMES = ("glow_rise_ms", "glow_fade_ms", "glow_drop_ms", "glow_half_ms", "grow_ms", "shrink_ms")
+LIMITS = {
+    "glow_peak": "luminance",
+    "glow_rise_ms": "time_ms",
+    "glow_fade_ms": "time_ms",
+    "glow_drop": "luminance",
+    "glow_drop_ms": "time_ms",
+    "glow_level": "luminance",
+    "glow_half_ms": "time_ms",
+    "grow_w": "bbox_pt",
+    "grow_h": "bbox_pt",
+    "grow_ms": "time_ms",
+    "shrink_ms": "time_ms",
+    "stretch": "ratio",
+    "centre": "centre_pt",
+}
+
+
+def rest_index(times, down):
+    before = [i for i, t in enumerate(times) if t < down]
+    return before[-1] if before else 0
+
+
+def rise(times, values, indices, peak, down):
+    if peak is None or not indices:
+        return None
+    for i in indices:
+        if values[i] >= PEAK_SHARE * peak:
+            return (times[i] - down) * 1000
+    return None
+
+
+def settle(times, values, indices, band, up):
+    if not indices:
+        return None
+    outside = [i for i in indices if abs(values[i]) > band]
+    if not outside:
+        return (times[indices[0]] - up) * 1000
+    last = outside[-1]
+    later = [i for i in indices if i > last]
+    return (times[later[0]] - up) * 1000 if later else None
+
+
+def drop(times, values, during, after, up):
+    if not after:
+        return None, None, None
+    chain = ([during[-1]] if during else []) + after
+    best, when, level = 0.0, None, None
+    for previous, current in zip(chain, chain[1:]):
+        fall = values[previous] - values[current]
+        if fall > best:
+            best, when, level = fall, (times[current] - up) * 1000, values[previous]
+    return best, when, level
+
+
+def half(times, values, after, peak, floor, up):
+    target = (peak + floor) / 2
+    for i in after:
+        if values[i] <= target:
+            return (times[i] - up) * 1000
+    return None
+
+
+def features(times, rows, window, end=math.inf):
+    down, up = window
+    times = list(times)
+    first = rest_index(times, down)
+    rest = rows[first]
+    during = [i for i, t in enumerate(times) if down <= t <= up]
+    after = [i for i, t in enumerate(times) if up < t < end]
+    glow = np.array([row["glow"] - rest["glow"] for row in rows], dtype=np.float64)
+    dw = np.array([row["width"] - rest["width"] for row in rows], dtype=np.float64)
+    dh = np.array([row["height"] - rest["height"] for row in rows], dtype=np.float64)
+    peak = float(glow[during].max()) if during else 0.0
+    lit = peak >= MIN_GLOW
+    grow_w = float(dw[during].max()) if during else 0.0
+    grow_h = float(dh[during].max()) if during else 0.0
+    grown = grow_w >= MIN_GROWTH
+    fall, fall_ms, level = drop(times, glow, during, after, up)
+    cut = lit and fall is not None and fall >= MIN_GLOW
+    ratio0 = rest["width"] / rest["height"] if rest["height"] > 0 else float("nan")
+    moving = during + after
+    stretch = [row["width"] / row["height"] / ratio0 - 1 for row in (rows[i] for i in moving) if row["height"] > 0 and row["width"] > 0]
+    offsets = [math.hypot(rows[i]["cx"] - rest["cx"], rows[i]["cy"] - rest["cy"]) for i in moving if np.isfinite(rows[i]["cx"]) and np.isfinite(rows[i]["cy"])]
+    return {
+        "hole": bool(takecheck.span_holes(times, row_changes(rows), [(down, up)], tail=min(takecheck.TAIL, end - up))),
+        "rest": {key: rest[key] for key in ("glow", "width", "height", "cx", "cy")},
+        "glow_peak": peak,
+        "glow_rise_ms": rise(times, glow, during, peak, down) if lit else None,
+        "glow_fade_ms": settle(times, glow, after, SETTLE_SHARE * peak, up) if lit else None,
+        "glow_drop": fall if lit else 0.0,
+        "glow_drop_ms": fall_ms if cut else None,
+        "glow_level": float(level) if cut else None,
+        "glow_half_ms": half(times, glow, after, peak, level if cut else 0.0, up) if lit else None,
+        "grow_w": grow_w,
+        "grow_h": grow_h,
+        "grow_ms": rise(times, dw, during, grow_w, down) if grown else None,
+        "shrink_ms": settle(times, dw, after, max(SETTLE_SHARE * grow_w, SIZE_BAND), up) if grown else None,
+        "stretch": max(stretch, key=abs) if stretch else 0.0,
+        "centre": max(offsets) if offsets else 0.0,
+    }
+
+
+GLOW_CHANGE = 0.5
+SIZE_CHANGE = 0.5
+
+
+def row_changes(rows):
+    found = [False]
+    for previous, current in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
+        moved = abs(current["glow"] - previous["glow"]) > GLOW_CHANGE
+        for key in ("width", "height", "cx", "cy"):
+            a, b = previous[key], current[key]
+            if np.isfinite(a) and np.isfinite(b) and abs(b - a) > SIZE_CHANGE:
+                moved = True
+        found.append(moved)
+    return found
+
+
+def difference(a, b):
+    if a is None and b is None:
+        return 0.0
+    if a is None or b is None:
+        return float("inf")
+    return float(abs(a - b))
+
+
+def compare(a, b):
+    holed = a.get("hole") or b.get("hole")
+    entry = {measure: float("nan") if holed and measure in TIMES else difference(a[measure], b[measure]) for measure in MEASURES}
+    entry["native"] = a
+    entry["flutter"] = b
+    return entry
+
+
+def step_features(scene, capture):
+    windows = touch.step_windows(scene.steps, capture.get("touches", []))
+    found = {}
+    ordered = sorted(windows.items())
+    for position, (index, group) in enumerate(ordered):
+        window = (group[0][0], group[-1][1])
+        end = ordered[position + 1][1][0][0] if position + 1 < len(ordered) else math.inf
+        found[f"step{index}"] = {
+            name: features(rows["times"], rows["rows"], window, end)
+            for name, rows in capture.get("rows", {}).items()
+            if rows["rows"] and "glow" in rows["rows"][0]
+        }
+    return found
+
+
+def compare_steps(scene, native, flutter):
+    a, b = step_features(scene, native), step_features(scene, flutter)
+    found = {}
+    for label in sorted(set(a) & set(b)):
+        found[label] = {name: compare(a[label][name], b[label][name]) for name in sorted(set(a[label]) & set(b[label]))}
+    return found
