```diff
diff --git a/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/takecheck.py b/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/takecheck.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..e37309f697bfabc723d32ed6af4239eee7b2140c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/takecheck.py
@@ -0,0 +1,135 @@
+import json
+import math
+import re
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import numpy as np
+from PIL import Image
+
+import metrics
+import touch
+import track
+
+HOLE_GAP = 0.0045
+LOST_GAP = 0.030
+ONSET = 0.090
+HOLD_GAP = 0.060
+TAIL = 1.2
+CHANGE = 0.2
+PTS = re.compile(r"pts_time:([0-9.]+)")
+TOLERANCE = 0.2
+RESULT = "takecheck.json"
+PRESS_KINDS = ("press", "pressDrag")
+
+
+def judged(scene):
+    return any(next(iter(step)) in PRESS_KINDS for step in scene.steps)
+
+
+def scripted(step):
+    kind, value = next(iter(step.items()))
+    if kind == "press":
+        return float(value["duration"])
+    if kind == "pressDrag":
+        target = value["to"] if isinstance(value["to"], dict) else {}
+        distance = math.hypot(target.get("dx", 0.0), target.get("dy", 0.0))
+        velocity = value.get("velocity")
+        travel = distance / velocity if velocity else 0.0
+        return float(value.get("pressDuration", 0.0)) + travel + float(value.get("hold", 0.0))
+    return None
+
+
+def span_holes(times, changing, windows, everywhere=False, tail=TAIL):
+    found = []
+    for k, (down, up) in enumerate(windows):
+        end = windows[k + 1][0] if k + 1 < len(windows) else up + tail
+        inside = [i for i, t in enumerate(times) if down <= t < end]
+        gaps = [(inside[j], inside[j + 1], times[inside[j + 1]] - times[inside[j]]) for j in range(len(inside) - 1)]
+        if gaps and gaps[0][2] < HOLE_GAP:
+            found.append(("squeezed first frame", times[gaps[0][1]]))
+        for (a, b, first), (_, c, second) in zip(gaps, gaps[1:]):
+            if first < HOLE_GAP and second < HOLE_GAP:
+                found.append(("burst", times[b]))
+                break
+        for (a, b, gap), (_, after, _) in zip(gaps, gaps[1:]):
+            held = down + ONSET <= times[a] < up
+            if round(gap, 4) > LOST_GAP and changing[a] and changing[after] and (everywhere or not held or round(gap, 4) > HOLD_GAP):
+                found.append((f"{gap * 1000:.1f} ms lost while the glass moved", times[b]))
+                break
+    return found
+
+
+def changes(values):
+    values = [np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float32) for v in values]
+    return [False] + [bool(np.abs(b - a).mean() > CHANGE) for a, b in zip(values, values[1:])]
+
+
+def region_frames(video, rect, dest):
+    dest = Path(dest)
+    dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for old in dest.glob("*.png"):
+        old.unlink()
+    x, y, w, h = rect
+    process = subprocess.run(
+        ["ffmpeg", "-loglevel", "info", "-y", "-i", str(video), "-fps_mode", "passthrough", "-vf", f"crop={w}:{h}:{x}:{y},scale={w // metrics.SCALE}:{h // metrics.SCALE},format=gray,showinfo", str(dest / "%06d.png")],
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        check=True,
+    )
+    times = [float(t) for t in PTS.findall(process.stderr)]
+    paths = sorted(dest.glob("*.png"))
+    count = min(len(paths), len(times))
+    return times[:count], [np.asarray(Image.open(path), dtype=np.float32) for path in paths[:count]]
+
+
+def app_of(take):
+    return "flutter" if Path(take).name.startswith("flutter") else "native"
+
+
+def failures(steps, windows, times, changing=None, app="native"):
+    found = []
+    expected = touch.expected_touches(steps)
+    if len(windows) != expected:
+        found.append(f"touches {len(windows)} against {expected}")
+    groups = touch.step_windows(steps, windows)
+    for index, group in sorted(groups.items()):
+        duration = scripted(steps[index])
+        if duration is None:
+            continue
+        actual = group[-1][1] - group[0][0]
+        if abs(actual - duration) > TOLERANCE * duration:
+            found.append(f"step {index} touch {actual:.3f} s against {duration:.3f} s")
+    if changing is None:
+        changing = [True] * len(times)
+    found += [f"capture hole ({kind}) at {at:.3f} s" for kind, at in span_holes(times, changing, windows, app == "flutter")]
+    return found
+
+
+def check(scene, take, cache=None):
+    take = Path(take)
+    root = Path(cache) if cache else take
+    frames = track.extract(take / "video.mp4", touch.marker_rect(), root / "marker")
+    windows = touch.touches(touch.phases(frames))
+    rect = track.pixel_rect(metrics.union([tuple(scene.regions[name]) for name in scene.track]))
+    times, pictures = region_frames(take / "video.mp4", rect, root / "takecheck")
+    found = failures(scene.steps, windows, times, changes(pictures), app_of(take))
+    result = {"scene": scene.id, "touches": [[round(a, 3), round(b, 3)] for a, b in windows], "failures": found}
+    if cache is None:
+        (take / RESULT).write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=1))
+    return result
+
+
+def read(take):
+    path = Path(take) / RESULT
+    return json.loads(path.read_text()) if path.exists() else None
+
+
+def exclude(take, run_dir, result):
+    take = Path(take)
+    target = Path(run_dir) / "excluded" / take.relative_to(run_dir)
+    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    shutil.move(str(take), str(target))
+    (target / "reason.txt").write_text("\n".join(result["failures"]) + "\n")
+    return target

--- a/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/track.py

@@ -106,6 +106,13 @@ def shape_row(frame, bare, edge_map, origin, scale=metrics.SCALE):
     }
 
 
+def glow(frame, rest_px):
+    if rest_px is None:
+        return float(metrics.luma(frame).mean())
+    left, top, right, bottom = rest_px
+    return float(metrics.luma(frame[top : bottom + 1, left : right + 1]).mean())
+
+
 def laplacian(image):
     gray = metrics.luma(image)
     centre = gray[1:-1, 1:-1] * 4 - gray[:-2, 1:-1] - gray[2:, 1:-1] - gray[1:-1, :-2] - gray[1:-1, 2:]
```

### Task 2: N3, N4 and held-out size scenes, judged on the press measures

**Files:**
- Modify: `tool/glass_lab/native/GlassLab/MaterialScenes.swift` (`NeighboursScene`, twelve registry lines)
- Modify: `tool/glass_lab/scenes.json` (`material.drag.{fast,out,far,up}`, `material.neighbours.{0,10,40}` with their pinned light rects, `material.press.{circle44,250x150,300x250,120x250,capsule36}`, and the press measures on every press scene)
- Test: `tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_scenes_2b.py`

**Why.** Rulings 3, 4, 8, 17 and 19. The drags reuse `InteractiveScene` (v13's 250 × 88 block); the neighbours are v15's two 120 × 88 glasses in `GlassEffectContainer(spacing: 20)` with v13's content, the left one pressed. The neighbour's region and light rect start 32 pt past its facing edge (law growth plus `spacing`), so the pressed glass and the merge bridge never enter them (F8). The 44 × 44 circle and the 120 × 36 capsule are Operator's sizes; the circle is held out of the fit (F11, F12), and so is the 120 × 250 portrait capsule, which separates width from height (ruling 8); the 250 × 150 and 300 × 250 rects are fitted. The new scenes run on `photo` only (ruling 19).

### Task 3: D5 B: `run` and `repeat` check every press take, re-record a holed one, and stop on a floor one take sets

**Files:**
- Modify: `tool/glass_lab/harness/lab.py` (`capture_checked`, `record_checked`, `leverage`, `noise-sources.json`), `analyze.py` (the `capture.<app>` gate)
- Test: `tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_takecheck.py`, `test_lab.py` (the pair-naming test counts folders and reads the sources file)

**Why.** Rulings 16 and 18, decisions D3 and D5 (taken). `run` re-records a press take that fails the take check up to twice and keeps the failed ones under `excluded/`; the report gates every press case on its kept takes' checks. `repeat` checks takes already on disk, moves failing ones to `excluded/`, records until enough pass, writes which pair of takes sets every limit, and, on a case with five or more takes, exits non-zero when one take sets a time limit above 3 × the median of the pairs without it, after writing every other case's floor and nothing for that case (R3).

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the failing tests.**

```diff
diff --git a/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_takecheck.py b/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_takecheck.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..b452155078ced86e45b61995e18971511b329a85
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_takecheck.py
@@ -0,0 +1,273 @@
+import contextlib
+import io
+import json
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+from unittest import mock
+
+sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
+
+import lab
+import manifest
+import takecheck
+
+SCENES = {s.id: s for s in manifest.load()}
+PRESS = [{"wait": 0.5}, {"press": {"at": "glass", "duration": 1.0}}, {"wait": 1.0}, {"pressDrag": {"from": "glass", "to": {"element": "glass", "dx": 60}, "pressDuration": 0.3, "velocity": 120, "hold": 0.5}}]
+
+
+def frames(start, end, step=1 / 60):
+    count = int(round((end - start) / step))
+    return [start + k * step for k in range(count + 1)]
+
+
+class TakeRuleTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_the_scripted_duration_of_a_press_and_of_a_press_drag(self):
+        self.assertEqual(takecheck.scripted(PRESS[1]), 1.0)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(takecheck.scripted(PRESS[3]), 0.3 + 0.5 + 0.5, places=9)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(takecheck.scripted({"pressDrag": {"from": "glass", "to": {"element": "glass", "dy": 300}, "pressDuration": 0.3, "velocity": 120, "hold": 0.5}}), 3.3, places=9)
+        self.assertIsNone(takecheck.scripted({"tap": "glass"}))
+
+    def test_a_clean_take_passes(self):
+        times = frames(0.0, 6.0)
+        self.assertEqual(takecheck.failures(PRESS, [(1.0, 2.0), (3.0, 4.3)], times), [])
+
+    def test_a_burst_anywhere_from_touch_down_to_the_next_touch_is_a_capture_hole(self):
+        windows = [(1.0, 2.0), (3.0, 4.3)]
+        burst = sorted(frames(0.0, 6.0) + [2.0 + 1 / 60 + 0.0017, 2.0 + 1 / 60 + 0.0034])
+        self.assertEqual(takecheck.failures(PRESS, windows, burst), ["capture hole (burst) at 2.018 s"])
+        at_the_cut = sorted(frames(0.0, 6.0) + [4.3 + 0.45 + 0.0017, 4.3 + 0.45 + 0.0034])
+        self.assertEqual(takecheck.failures(PRESS, windows, at_the_cut), ["capture hole (burst) at 4.752 s"])
+        after = sorted(frames(0.0, 6.0) + [5.7 + 0.0017, 5.7 + 0.0034])
+        self.assertEqual(takecheck.failures(PRESS, windows, after), [])
+
+    def test_the_archived_dark_stripes_release_is_a_hole_with_its_lost_frames_and_its_burst(self):
+        up = 23.955
+        hold = [up - 0.0167 * k for k in range(8, 0, -1)] + [up]
+        release = [(0.005, False), (0.080, True), (0.1017, True), (0.0483, True), (0.005, False), (0.010, False), (0.005, True), (0.015, True)]
+        release += [(0.0033, False), (0.0033, False), (0.005, False)] + [(0.0167, False)] * 20
+        times, changing = list(hold), [False] * len(hold)
+        for gap, moved in release:
+            times.append(times[-1] + gap)
+            changing.append(moved)
+        found = takecheck.span_holes(times, changing, [(up - 1.0, up)])
+        self.assertEqual([kind for kind, _ in found], ["burst", "101.7 ms lost while the glass moved"])

+    def test_native_s_slow_cadence_after_its_onset_is_not_lost_and_the_same_gaps_in_flutter_are(self):

+        found = takecheck.span_holes(times, changing, [(down, 2.0)], everywhere=True)

+    def test_a_gap_while_the_glass_moves_in_native_s_onset_is_lost(self):

+class CheckedRecordingTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        quiet = contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO())
+        quiet.__enter__()
+        self.addCleanup(quiet.__exit__, None, None, None)
+
+    def test_run_records_a_failing_press_take_again_and_keeps_the_failed_one(self):
+        scene = SCENES["material.interactive"]
+        results = iter([{"failures": ["capture hole"]}, {"failures": []}])
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
+            case = Path(temp) / "case"
+
+            def capture(udid, scene, app, backdrop, take, target):
+                take.mkdir(parents=True)
+
+            lab.capture_checked("udid", scene, "native", "photo", case, capture=capture, check=lambda scene, take: next(results))
+            self.assertTrue((case / "native").is_dir())
+            self.assertEqual((case / "excluded" / "native-0" / "reason.txt").read_text(), "capture hole\n")
+
+    def test_run_gives_up_after_its_retries_and_keeps_the_last_take(self):
+        scene = SCENES["material.interactive"]
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
+            case = Path(temp) / "case"
+            calls = []
+
+            def capture(udid, scene, app, backdrop, take, target):
+                calls.append(take)
+                take.mkdir(parents=True)
+
+            lab.capture_checked("udid", scene, "native", "photo", case, capture=capture, check=lambda scene, take: {"failures": ["hole"]})
+            self.assertEqual(len(calls), lab.RETRIES + 1)
+            self.assertTrue((case / "native").is_dir())
+
+    def test_repeat_excludes_failing_takes_and_records_until_enough_pass(self):
+        scene = SCENES["material.interactive"]
+        verdicts = iter([["hole"], [], ["squeezed"], []])
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
+            run = Path(temp)
+            folder = run / "takes" / scene.id / "dark-photo"
+
+            def capture(udid, scene, app, backdrop, take):
+                take.mkdir(parents=True)
+
+            def check(scene, take):
+                found = {"failures": next(verdicts)}
+                (take / takecheck.RESULT).write_text(json.dumps(found))
+                return found
+
+            lab.record_checked("udid", scene, "photo", folder, run, 2, capture=capture, check=check)
+            self.assertEqual(lab.take_numbers(folder), [1, 3])
+            self.assertEqual(lab.take_numbers(run / "excluded" / "takes" / scene.id / "dark-photo"), [0, 2])
+
+    def test_repeat_checks_takes_already_on_disk_before_recomputing(self):
+        scene = SCENES["material.interactive"]
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
+            run = Path(temp)
+            folder = run / "takes" / scene.id / "dark-photo"
+            for number in range(3):
+                (folder / str(number)).mkdir(parents=True)
+
+            def check(scene, take):
+                found = {"failures": ["hole"] if take.name == "1" else []}
+                (take / takecheck.RESULT).write_text(json.dumps(found))
+                return found
+
+            lab.record_checked("udid", scene, "photo", folder, run, 0, capture=mock.Mock(), check=check)
+            self.assertEqual(lab.take_numbers(folder), [0, 2])
+
+
+class LeverageStopTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @staticmethod

+

+
+    def test_the_leverage_check_waits_for_five_takes(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.noise_with(4), {})
+        self.assertEqual(self.noise_with(5)["glass.step1.press.glow_rise_ms"]["take"], "3")
+
+    def test_a_stopped_case_keeps_its_old_floor_and_repeat_exits_after_writing_the_others(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
+            run = Path(temp) / "run"
+            noise_file = Path(temp) / "noise.json"
+            noise_file.write_text(json.dumps({"material.interactive": {"dark-photo": {"old": 1.0}, "light-photo": {"old": 2.0}}}))
+
+            def fake_record(udid, scene, backdrop, folder, run_dir, times):
+                for number in range(5):

+
+            def fake_noise(scene, takes, case_root):

+                flagged = {"x.glow_rise_ms": {"take": "3", "noise": 60.0, "median_without": 10.0}} if case_root.name == "dark-photo" else {}
+                (case_root / "noise-sources.json").write_text(json.dumps({"sources": {}, "leverage": flagged}))
+                return {"new": 3.0}, 0.0
+
+            args = mock.Mock(scene="material.interactive", times=0, appearance=None, backdrop="photo", a11y="none", into=str(run))
+            with mock.patch.object(lab.build, "require_fresh"), mock.patch.object(lab.sim, "device", return_value="udid"), \
+                 mock.patch.object(lab.sim, "accessibility"), mock.patch.object(lab.sim, "appearance"), \
+                 mock.patch.object(lab, "record_checked", side_effect=fake_record), mock.patch.object(lab, "case_noise", side_effect=fake_noise), \
+                 mock.patch.object(lab, "NOISE", noise_file), contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
+                with self.assertRaises(SystemExit) as stopped:
+                    lab.cmd_repeat(args)
+            written = json.loads(noise_file.read_text())["material.interactive"]
+            self.assertIn("take 3", str(stopped.exception))
+            self.assertEqual(written["dark-photo"], {"old": 1.0})
+            self.assertEqual(written["light-photo"], {"new": 3.0})
+
+
+class LeverageTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_one_take_that_sets_a_time_limit_above_three_times_its_siblings_is_flagged(self):
+        values = {("0", "1"): 10.0, ("0", "2"): 12.0, ("1", "2"): 8.0, ("0", "3"): 60.0, ("1", "3"): 58.0, ("2", "3"): 55.0}
+        found = lab.leverage({"glass.step1.press.glow_rise_ms": values}, 17.0)
+        self.assertEqual(found["glass.step1.press.glow_rise_ms"]["take"], "3")

+        self.assertEqual(lab.leverage({"x.glow_rise_ms": small}, 17.0), {})
+        self.assertEqual(lab.leverage({"x.grow_w": {("0", "1"): 9.0, ("0", "2"): 0.1, ("1", "2"): 0.1}}, 17.0), {})
```

```diff
+def leverage(pairs, threshold):
+    found = {}
+    for name, values in pairs.items():
+        if not name.endswith("_ms") or not values:
+            continue
+        (a, b), worst = max(values.items(), key=lambda item: item[1])
+        if worst * analyze.NOISE_FACTOR <= threshold:
+            continue
+        for take in (a, b):
+            rest = sorted(v for (i, j), v in values.items() if take not in (i, j))
+            if rest and worst > LEVERAGE * rest[len(rest) // 2]:
+                found[name] = {"take": take, "noise": worst, "median_without": rest[len(rest) // 2]}
+    return found
+

+    if leveraged:
+        raise SystemExit("one take sets a time limit above 3 x its siblings' median: " + "; ".join(leveraged))
 
 
 def cmd_reboot(args):
```

### Task 4: `pressfit`: fit the press model on clean native series and write `ios27_press.dart`

**Files:**
- Create: `tool/glass_lab/harness/pressfit.py` (`profile` and `fit` commands)
- Test: `tool/glass_lab/harness/tests/test_pressfit.py`

**Why.** Rulings 5 to 9. Every press number the package ships comes from this tool. It skips takes that fail the take check or hole their first press for every timing fit, holds the 44 × 44 circle and the 120 × 250 capsule out, fits the size law on the settled growth of every take, holed or not, as a function of height and width, keeps only a form within 1 pt of native at every measured size and chooses among those by leave-one-size-out error, fits the light on every backdrop with the glow width from rings inside the glass, reads the cut per size, and refuses a law or sigma on its grid edge, more than 25 % of per-take spring fits on their grid edge, a Reduce Motion cut from fewer than five presses, or a missing fit.

```diff
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import numpy as np
+
+sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
+
+import pressfit
+import springfit
+
+
+def case(height, grow):
+    return {"size": (250.0, height, "capsule"), "features": {"grow_w": grow * 1.09}, "plateau": grow}
+
+
+def spring_case(height, settled, response=0.26, damping=0.615):
+    times = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.arange(0.5, 1.5 + 1e-9, 1 / 120)])
+    shares = np.where(times >= 0.55, springfit.step_response(np.maximum(times - 0.55, 0.0), response, damping), 0.0)
+    rows = {"times": times.tolist(), "width": (250.0 + settled * shares).tolist()}
+    found = {"key": "k", "times": times, "rows": rows, "down": 0.5, "up": 1.5, "size": (250.0, height, "capsule")}
+    found["plateau"] = pressfit.plateau(found)
+    found["features"] = {"grow_w": float(settled * shares.max())}
+    return found
+
+
+class PressFitTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_a_height_law_is_recovered_with_its_cap_and_no_width_term(self):
+        cases = [case(h, min(16.0, 1000.0 / h)) for h in (44, 53, 58, 88, 120, 200)]
+        found = pressfit.fit_growth_law(cases)
+        height = found["forms"]["height"]
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(height["cap"], 16.0, places=2)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(height["at_100"], 10.0, places=2)
+        self.assertEqual(height["power"], 1.0)
+        self.assertLess(height["rms"], 1e-6)
+        self.assertEqual(pressfit.law_value(height, 250, 44), 16.0)
+        self.assertEqual(pressfit.law_value(height, 44, 44), 16.0)
+        self.assertTrue(found["within"])
+
+    def test_a_law_in_width_and_height_is_recovered_on_portrait_and_landscape_sizes(self):
+        sizes = ((58, 58), (138, 53), (250, 44), (250, 88), (300, 120), (250, 150), (360, 200), (300, 250), (120, 250), (88, 200), (360, 100))
+        law = lambda w, h: min(16.0, 9.0 * (100.0 / h) ** 1.2 * (100.0 / w) ** 0.3)
+        cases = [{"size": (float(w), float(h), "capsule"), "features": {}, "plateau": law(w, h)} for w, h in sizes]
+        found = pressfit.fit_growth_law(cases)
+        both = found["forms"]["height_width"]
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(both["power"], 1.2, delta=0.011)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(both["width_power"], 0.3, delta=0.051)
+        self.assertFalse(found["forms"]["height"]["within"])
+        self.assertEqual(found["chosen"], "height_width")
+        self.assertGreater(pressfit.law_value(both, 120, 250), pressfit.law_value(both, 300, 250))
+        self.assertGreater(pressfit.law_value(both, 360, 100), pressfit.law_value(both, 100, 360))
+
+    def test_the_law_is_fitted_on_the_settled_growth_so_a_spring_overshoot_is_not_counted_twice(self):
+        cases = [spring_case(h, min(16.0, 1000.0 / h)) for h in (44, 53, 58, 88, 120, 200)]
+        self.assertGreater(cases[0]["features"]["grow_w"], 16.0 * 1.05)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(cases[0]["plateau"], 16.0, delta=0.05)
+        found = pressfit.fit_growth_law(cases)["forms"]["height"]
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["cap"], 16.0, delta=0.06)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["at_100"], 10.0, delta=0.05)
+
+    def test_the_glow_rings_leave_out_pixels_outside_the_glass(self):
+        scale = 3
+        height, width = 88 * scale, 250 * scale
+        change = np.full((height, width), 20.0)
+        ys, xs = np.mgrid[0:height, 0:width]
+        x, y = (xs + 0.5) / scale - 125, (ys + 0.5) / scale - 44
+        change[~pressfit.inside_shape(x, y, 250, 88, "capsule")] = 0.0
+        found = pressfit.rings(change, 250, 88, "capsule", scale)
+        self.assertTrue(all(abs(value - 20.0) < 1e-9 for _, value in found))
+        self.assertGreater(max(d for d, _ in found), 120)
+
+    def test_the_cut_is_read_per_size_and_a_size_without_one_takes_its_nearest(self):
+        fits = [
+            ({"size": (58.0, 58.0, "circle")}, {"hold_level": 11.0, "swell": 40.0, "cut_ms": 620.0}),
+            ({"size": (250.0, 88.0, "capsule")}, {"hold_level": 11.0, "swell": 9.0, "cut_ms": 405.0}),
+            ({"size": (360.0, 200.0, "rect32")}, {"hold_level": 0.0, "swell": 5.0, "cut_ms": None}),
+        ]
+        table = pressfit.size_table(fits, 86.0)
+        self.assertEqual([row["cut_ms"] for row in table], [620.0, 405.0, 405.0])
+        self.assertEqual(table[2]["cut_from"], [250.0, 88.0])
+
+    def test_problems_name_spring_fits_on_the_grid_edge_and_a_thin_reduce_motion_cut(self):
+        summary = {
+            "law": {"on_edge": False},
+            "growth": {"response": 0.26},
+            "release": {"response": 0.33},
+            "glow": {"rise_response": 0.06, "hold_ms": 57.0, "fade_response": 0.38, "cut_ms": 422.0, "reduce_motion_cut_ms": 570.0, "reduce_motion_presses": 3},
+            "sigma": {"on_edge": False},
+            "sizes": [{}],
+            "edge_share": 0.5,
+        }
+        found = pressfit.problems(summary)
+        self.assertTrue(any("grid edge" in p for p in found))
+        self.assertTrue(any("3 presses" in p for p in found))
+        clean = dict(summary, edge_share=0.1, glow=dict(summary["glow"], reduce_motion_presses=5))
+        self.assertEqual(pressfit.problems(clean), [])
+
+    def test_a_form_that_misses_a_measured_size_by_more_than_the_size_threshold_is_not_chosen(self):
+        sizes = ((58, 58), (138, 53), (250, 44), (250, 88), (250, 88), (250, 88), (300, 120), (250, 150), (360, 200), (300, 250))
+        cases = [{"size": (float(w), float(h), "capsule"), "features": {}, "plateau": min(15.84, 9.69 * (100.0 / h) ** 1.6)} for w, h in sizes]
+        found = pressfit.fit_growth_law(cases)
+        self.assertFalse(found["forms"]["area"]["within"])
+        self.assertTrue(found["forms"]["height"]["within"])
+        self.assertIn(found["chosen"], ("height", "height_width"))
+        self.assertTrue(all(abs(r) <= pressfit.SIZE_LIMIT for r in found["residuals"].values()))
+
+    def test_problems_name_a_law_with_a_height_outside_the_size_threshold(self):
+        summary = {
+            "law": {"on_edge": False, "within": False},
+            "growth": {"response": 0.26},
+            "release": {"response": 0.33},
+            "glow": {"rise_response": 0.06, "hold_ms": 57.0, "fade_response": 0.38, "cut_ms": 422.0, "reduce_motion_cut_ms": 570.0, "reduce_motion_presses": 5},
+            "sigma": {"on_edge": False},
+            "sizes": [{}],
+            "edge_share": 0.1,
+        }
+        self.assertTrue(any("within" in p for p in pressfit.problems(summary)))
+
+    def test_the_law_reads_every_take_s_plateau_holed_or_not(self):
+        times = [0.0] + [0.5 + k / 60 for k in range(70)]
+        width = [250.0] + [262.0] * 70
+        entry = lambda hole: {
+            "touches": [[0.5, 1.6]],
+            "takecheck": {"failures": ["capture hole"] if hole else []},
+            "steps": {"step1": {"glass": {"hole": hole}}},
+            "series": {"glass": {"times": times, "width": width, "height": [88.0] * 71, "glow": [100.0] * 71, "cx": [201.0] * 71, "cy": [451.0] * 71}},
+        }
+        series = {"material.interactive/dark-photo/a": entry(True), "material.interactive/light-photo/b": entry(False), "material.interactive/dark-stripes/c": entry(False)}
+        found = list(pressfit.plateaus(series))
+        self.assertEqual(sorted(c["key"] for c in found), ["material.interactive/dark-photo/a", "material.interactive/light-photo/b"])
+        self.assertTrue(all(abs(c["plateau"] - 12.0) < 1e-9 for c in found))
+        self.assertEqual([c["key"] for c in pressfit.presses(series)], ["material.interactive/light-photo/b", "material.interactive/dark-stripes/c"])
+
+    def test_the_glow_profile_is_the_median_of_its_takes(self):
+        profiles = [{"take": str(k), "box_pt": [250, 88], "shape": "capsule", "profile": [(0.5, 20.0 + k), (1.5, 18.0 + k)], "hold_level": 11.0 + k} for k in range(3)]
+        found = pressfit.median_profile(profiles)
+        self.assertEqual(found["profile"], [(0.5, 21.0), (1.5, 19.0)])
+        self.assertEqual(found["hold_level"], 12.0)
+
+    def test_a_profile_key_names_its_native_take(self):
+        self.assertEqual(pressfit.profile_take("runs", "material.interactive/dark-photo-reduce-motion/20261009-085013"), Path("runs/20261009-085013/material.interactive/dark-photo-reduce-motion/native"))
+
+    def test_a_spring_is_recovered_without_renormalising_its_end(self):
+        t = np.arange(0.0, 0.4, 1 / 120)
+        values = springfit.step_response(t, 0.3, 0.7)
+        found = pressfit.fit_raw(t, values)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["response"], 0.3, places=2)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["damping"], 0.7, places=2)
+
+    def test_a_glow_that_jumps_then_settles_is_fitted_with_its_jump(self):
+        t = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.arange(1 / 120, 0.6, 1 / 120)])
+        values = 0.6 + 0.4 * springfit.step_response(t, 0.2, 1.0)
+        values[0] = 0.0
+        found = pressfit.fit_jump(t, values)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["jump"], 0.6, places=2)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["response"], 0.2, places=2)
+
+    def test_the_reach_is_the_time_the_spring_takes_to_a_share(self):
+        spring = {"response": 0.3, "damping": 1.0}
+        reached = pressfit.reach(spring, 0.5)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(float(springfit.step_response(reached, 0.3, 1.0)), 0.5, delta=0.01)
+
+    def test_the_glow_width_is_recovered_from_a_ring_profile(self):
+        profile = [(d + 0.5, 11.0 + 14.0 * np.exp(-((d + 0.5) ** 2) / (2 * 61.0**2))) for d in range(0, 120)]
+        found = pressfit.fit_sigma(profile, 11.0)
+        self.assertEqual(found["sigma"], 61.0)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(found["peak"], 14.0, places=3)
+
+    def test_a_gaussian_much_wider_than_the_glass_averages_to_one(self):
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(pressfit.gaussian_mean(10, 10, 1000.0, shape="circle"), 1.0, places=4)
+        self.assertLess(pressfit.gaussian_mean(360, 200, 61.0, shape="rect32"), pressfit.gaussian_mean(58, 58, 61.0, shape="circle"))
+
+    def test_write_refuses_a_summary_with_problems_and_writes_one_without(self):
+        summary = {
+            "law": {"cap": 16.78, "at_100": 10.15, "power": 1.0, "on_edge": False},
+            "growth": {"delay": 0.05, "response": 0.26, "damping": 0.6},
+            "release": {"delay": 0.02, "response": 0.33, "damping": 0.6},
+            "glow": {"rise_jump": 0.0, "rise_response": 0.06, "rise_damping": 1.9, "hold_ms": 57.0, "fade_response": 0.38, "fade_damping": 1.05, "cut_ms": 422.0, "reduce_motion_cut_ms": 570.0, "reduce_motion_presses": 5},
+            "sigma": {"sigma": 61.0, "on_edge": False},
+            "edge_share": 0.0,
+            "sizes": [{"area": 3364.0, "hold": 11.0, "swell_peak": 39.0, "cut_ms": 620.0}, {"area": 72000.0, "hold": 0.0, "swell_peak": 15.0, "cut_ms": 400.0}],
+        }
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
+            target = Path(folder) / "ios27_press.dart"
+            pressfit.write_table(summary, target)
+            text = target.read_text()
+            self.assertIn("const double ios27PressGrowthCap = 16.78;", text)
+            self.assertIn("const List<double> ios27PressGlowAreas = [3364.0, 72000.0];", text)
+            broken = dict(summary, glow=dict(summary["glow"], cut_ms=None))
+            with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
+                pressfit.write_table(broken, Path(folder) / "other.dart")
+            self.assertFalse((Path(folder) / "other.dart").exists())
+
+
+    def test_a_refit_where_no_form_keeps_every_size_within_the_threshold_writes_nothing(self):
+        grown = {(58, 58): 16.0, (250, 88): 11.0, (250, 120): 3.0, (250, 150): 9.0, (250, 200): 2.0, (300, 250): 7.0}
+        cases = [{"size": (float(w), float(h), "capsule"), "features": {}, "plateau": g} for (w, h), g in grown.items()]
+        law = pressfit.fit_growth_law(cases)
+        self.assertFalse(any(form["within"] for form in law["forms"].values()))
+        self.assertFalse(law["within"])
+        summary = {

+            "growth": {"delay": 0.05, "response": 0.26, "damping": 0.6},
+            "release": {"delay": 0.02, "response": 0.33, "damping": 0.6},
+            "glow": {"rise_jump": 0.0, "rise_response": 0.06, "rise_damping": 1.9, "hold_ms": 57.0, "fade_response": 0.38, "fade_damping": 1.05, "cut_ms": 422.0, "reduce_motion_cut_ms": 570.0, "reduce_motion_presses": 5},
+            "sigma": {"sigma": 61.0, "on_edge": False},

+
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Apply the implementation.**

Patch `t04-impl` (`c0e69d9a4..87b88ab02`, 1 files):

```diff
diff --git a/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/pressfit.py b/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/pressfit.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..fa7f5c656d23e07b10296b30b983e8ad2e3c8387
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/mobile/tool/glass_lab/harness/pressfit.py
@@ -0,0 +1,584 @@
+import json
+import math
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import numpy as np
+
+import metrics
+import press
+import springfit
+
+LAB = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+TABLE = LAB.parents[1] / "packages" / "ios_liquid_glass" / "lib" / "src" / "motion" / "ios27_press.dart"
+STEP_PT = 1.0 / 3.0
+SIGMAS = np.arange(20.0, 300.0 + 1e-9, 1.0)
+FOLLOWS = np.round(np.arange(0.05, 2.0 + 1e-9, 0.01), 2)
+PLATEAU_SECONDS = 0.3
+HELD_OUT = ("material.press.circle44", "material.press.120x250")
+EDGE_SHARE = 0.25
+SIZE_LIMIT = metrics.THRESHOLDS["bbox_pt"]
+REDUCE_MOTION_PRESSES = 5
+FIT_RESPONSES = np.round(np.arange(0.01, 1.5 + 1e-9, 0.005), 3)
+FIT_DAMPINGS = np.round(np.arange(0.1, 4.0 + 1e-9, 0.01), 2)
+HOLD_DROP = 0.5
+
+
+def load(paths):
+    found = {}
+    for path in paths:
+        found.update(json.loads(Path(path).read_text()))
+    return found
+
+
+def mode(key):
+    return "reduce-motion" if "reduce-motion" in key else "normal"
+
+
+def size_of(scene_id):
+    if scene_id == "material.interactive" or scene_id.startswith("material.drag."):
+        return 250.0, 88.0, "capsule"
+    tail = scene_id.split(".")[-1]
+    if tail.startswith("circle"):
+        side = float(tail[len("circle") :])
+        return side, side, "circle"
+    width, height = (float(v) for v in tail.split("x"))
+    return width, height, "rect32" if (width, height) in RECT32 else "capsule"
+
+
+RECT32 = ((360.0, 200.0), (300.0, 250.0))
+
+
+def usable(entry, name):
+    if entry.get("takecheck", {}).get("failures"):
+        return False
+    return not entry["steps"].get("step1", {}).get(name, {}).get("hole", False)
+
+
+def skipped(series):
+    return sorted(key for key, entry in series.items() if any(not usable(entry, name) for name in entry["series"]))
+
+
+def plateaus(series):
+    for key, entry in series.items():
+        scene_id = key.split("/")[0]
+        if not (scene_id == "material.interactive" or scene_id.startswith("material.press.")) or scene_id in HELD_OUT:
+            continue
+        if mode(key) != "normal" or key.split("/")[1].split("-")[1] != "photo" or not entry.get("touches"):
+            continue
+        down, up = entry["touches"][0]
+        for name, rows in entry["series"].items():
+            case = {"key": key, "size": size_of(scene_id), "times": np.array(rows["times"], dtype=np.float64), "rows": rows, "down": down, "up": up}
+            case["plateau"] = plateau(case)
+            if case["plateau"] is not None:
+                yield case
+
+
+def presses(series):
+    for key, entry in series.items():
+        scene_id = key.split("/")[0]
+        if not (scene_id == "material.interactive" or scene_id.startswith("material.press.")) or scene_id in HELD_OUT:
+            continue
+        for name, rows in entry["series"].items():
+            if not usable(entry, name) or "step1" not in entry["steps"]:
+                continue
+            times = np.array(rows["times"], dtype=np.float64)
+            down, up = entry["touches"][0]
+            later = [w[0] for w in entry["touches"] if w[0] > up]
+            case = {
+                "key": key,
+                "scene": scene_id,
+                "mode": mode(key),
+                "backdrop": key.split("/")[1].split("-")[1],
+                "size": size_of(scene_id),
+                "times": times,
+                "rows": rows,
+                "down": down,
+                "up": up,
+                "end": later[0] if later else math.inf,
+                "features": entry["steps"]["step1"][name],
+            }
+            case["plateau"] = plateau(case)
+            yield case
+
+
+def plateau(case):
+    times, rows = case["times"], case["rows"]
+    first = rest_index(times, case["down"])
+    width = np.array(rows["width"]) - rows["width"][first]
+    held = width[(times > case["up"] - PLATEAU_SECONDS) & (times <= case["up"])]
+    return float(held.mean()) if len(held) else None
+
+
+def rest_index(times, down):
+    return press.rest_index(list(times), down)
+
+
+LAW_CAPS = np.round(np.arange(14.0, 20.0 + 1e-9, 0.02), 2)
+LAW_AT_100 = np.round(np.arange(4.0, 40.0 + 1e-9, 0.01), 2)
+LAW_POWERS = np.round(np.arange(0.5, 2.0 + 1e-9, 0.01), 2)
+LAW_WIDTH_POWERS = np.round(np.arange(-1.0, 1.0 + 1e-9, 0.05), 2)
+LAW_FORMS = ("height", "area", "height_width")
+
+
+def law_errors(caps, scales, values, grown):
+    model = np.minimum(caps[:, None, None], scales[None, :, None] * values[None, None, :])
+    return np.sqrt(np.mean((model - grown[None, None, :]) ** 2, axis=2))
+
+
+def law_point(values, grown):
+    coarse_caps, coarse_scales = LAW_CAPS[::5], LAW_AT_100[::10]
+    errors = law_errors(coarse_caps, coarse_scales, values, grown)
+    i, j = np.unravel_index(np.argmin(errors), errors.shape)
+    caps = LAW_CAPS[np.abs(LAW_CAPS - coarse_caps[i]) <= 0.1 + 1e-9]
+    scales = LAW_AT_100[np.abs(LAW_AT_100 - coarse_scales[j]) <= 0.1 + 1e-9]
+    errors = law_errors(caps, scales, values, grown)
+    k, m = np.unravel_index(np.argmin(errors), errors.shape)
+    return float(errors[k, m]), float(caps[k]), float(scales[m])
+
+
+def law_grid(base, grown, powers, factor=None):
+    factor = np.ones_like(base) if factor is None else factor
+    best = None
+    for power in powers:
+        error, cap, at_100 = law_point(factor * base ** power, grown)
+        if best is None or error < best[0]:
+            best = (error, cap, at_100, float(power))
+    error, cap, at_100, power = best
+    return {
+        "cap": cap,
+        "at_100": at_100,
+        "power": power,
+        "rms": error,
+        "on_edge": bool(cap in (LAW_CAPS[0], LAW_CAPS[-1]) or at_100 in (LAW_AT_100[0], LAW_AT_100[-1]) or (len(powers) > 1 and power in (powers[0], powers[-1]))),
+    }
+
+
+def law_fit(form, widths, heights, grown):
+    if form == "height":
+        return dict(law_grid(100.0 / heights, grown, LAW_POWERS), width_power=0.0, form=form)
+    if form == "area":
+        law = law_grid(1e4 / (widths * heights), grown, LAW_POWERS)
+        return dict(law, width_power=law["power"], form=form)
+    best = None
+    for width_power in LAW_WIDTH_POWERS:
+        law = law_grid(100.0 / heights, grown, LAW_POWERS, (100.0 / widths) ** width_power)
+        if best is None or law["rms"] < best["rms"]:
+            best = dict(law, width_power=float(width_power), form=form)
+    best["on_edge"] = bool(best["on_edge"] or best["width_power"] in (LAW_WIDTH_POWERS[0], LAW_WIDTH_POWERS[-1]))
+    return best
+
+
+def law_value(law, width, height):
+    return min(law["cap"], law["at_100"] * (100.0 / height) ** law["power"] * (100.0 / width) ** law.get("width_power", 0.0))
+
+
+def held_out(form, widths, heights, grown, sizes):
+    squares = []
+    for size in sorted(set(sizes)):
+        keep = np.array([s != size for s in sizes])
+        law = law_fit(form, widths[keep], heights[keep], grown[keep])
+        squares += [(law_value(law, w, h) - g) ** 2 for w, h, g, s in zip(widths, heights, grown, sizes) if s == size]
+    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(squares)))
+
+
+def size_residuals(law, widths, heights, grown, sizes):
+    found = {}
+    for size in sorted(set(sizes)):
+        mask = np.array([s == size for s in sizes])
+        found[f"{size[0]:g}x{size[1]:g}"] = law_value(law, size[0], size[1]) - float(np.median(grown[mask]))
+    return found
+
+
+def fit_growth_law(cases, forms=LAW_FORMS):
+    widths = np.array([c["size"][0] for c in cases], dtype=np.float64)
+    heights = np.array([c["size"][1] for c in cases], dtype=np.float64)
+    grown = np.array([c["plateau"] for c in cases], dtype=np.float64)
+    sizes = [tuple(c["size"][:2]) for c in cases]
+    found = {}
+    for form in forms:
+        law = law_fit(form, widths, heights, grown)
+        residuals = size_residuals(law, widths, heights, grown, sizes)
+        found[form] = dict(law, held_out_rms=held_out(form, widths, heights, grown, sizes), residuals=residuals, within=all(abs(r) <= SIZE_LIMIT for r in residuals.values()))
+    eligible = [form for form in forms if found[form]["within"]]
+    if eligible:
+        chosen = min(eligible, key=lambda form: found[form]["held_out_rms"])
+    else:
+        chosen = min(found, key=lambda form: max(abs(r) for r in found[form]["residuals"].values()))
+    ranked = sorted(eligible, key=lambda form: found[form]["held_out_rms"])
+    separated = len(ranked) < 2 or found[ranked[1]]["held_out_rms"] - found[ranked[0]]["held_out_rms"] > SIZE_LIMIT / 3
+    return dict(found[chosen], chosen=chosen, forms=found, separated=separated, cases=len(cases))
+
+
+def fit_raw(times, values):
+    t = np.asarray(times, dtype=np.float64)[None, None, :]
+    curves = springfit.step_response(t, FIT_RESPONSES[:, None, None], FIT_DAMPINGS[None, :, None])
+    errors = np.sqrt(np.mean((curves - np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64)[None, None, :]) ** 2, axis=2))
+    i, j = np.unravel_index(np.argmin(errors), errors.shape)
+    edge = i in (0, len(FIT_RESPONSES) - 1) or j in (0, len(FIT_DAMPINGS) - 1)
+    return {"response": float(FIT_RESPONSES[i]), "damping": float(FIT_DAMPINGS[j]), "rms": float(errors[i, j]), "at_grid_edge": bool(edge)}
+
+
+def reach(spring, share):
+    t = np.arange(0.0, 1.0, 0.0005)
+    curve = springfit.step_response(t, spring["response"], spring["damping"])
+    hits = np.nonzero(curve >= share)[0]
+    return float(t[hits[0]]) if len(hits) else 0.0
+
+
+JUMPS = np.round(np.arange(0.0, 1.0 + 1e-9, 0.05), 2)
+
+
+def fit_jump(times, values):
+    t = np.asarray(times, dtype=np.float64)[1:]
+    v = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64)[1:]
+    if len(t) < 2:
+        return None
+    curves = springfit.step_response(t[None, None, :], FIT_RESPONSES[:, None, None], FIT_DAMPINGS[None, :, None])
+    best = None
+    for jump in JUMPS:
+        errors = np.sqrt(np.mean((jump + (1 - jump) * curves - v[None, None, :]) ** 2, axis=2))
+        i, j = np.unravel_index(np.argmin(errors), errors.shape)
+        if best is None or errors[i, j] < best["rms"]:
+            edge = i in (0, len(FIT_RESPONSES) - 1) or j in (0, len(FIT_DAMPINGS) - 1)
+            best = {"jump": float(jump), "response": float(FIT_RESPONSES[i]), "damping": float(FIT_DAMPINGS[j]), "rms": float(errors[i, j]), "at_grid_edge": bool(edge)}
+    return best
+
+
+def first_change(times, values, start, reference, threshold):
+    for t, v in zip(times, values):
+        if t > start and abs(v - reference) >= threshold:
+            return t
+    return None
+
+
+def shares(times, values, origin, stop, start_value, end_value, zero=None):
+    before = times[times < origin]
+    keep = (times >= origin) & (times <= stop)
+    if zero is None:
+        zero = before[-1] if len(before) else origin
+    t = np.concatenate([[0.0], times[keep] - zero])
+    v = np.concatenate([[start_value], values[keep]])
+    return t, (v - start_value) / (end_value - start_value)
+
+
+def growth_fit(case):
+    times, rows = case["times"], case["rows"]
+    first = rest_index(times, case["down"])
+    width = np.array(rows["width"]) - rows["width"][first]
+    plateau = width[(times > case["up"] - PLATEAU_SECONDS) & (times <= case["up"])]
+    if not len(plateau) or plateau.mean() < press.MIN_GROWTH:
+        return None
+    began = first_change(times, width, case["down"], 0.0, STEP_PT + 1e-6)
+    if began is None:
+        return None
+    level = float(plateau.mean())
+    t, v = shares(times, width, began, case["up"], 0.0, level)
+    spring = fit_raw(t, v)
+    return {"delay": float(began - case["down"] - reach(spring, STEP_PT / level)), "seen_ms": float((began - case["down"]) * 1000), **spring}
+
+
+def release_fit(case):
+    times, rows = case["times"], case["rows"]
+    first = rest_index(times, case["down"])
+    width = np.array(rows["width"]) - rows["width"][first]
+    at_up = float(width[times <= case["up"]][-1])
+    if at_up < press.MIN_GROWTH:
+        return None
+    began = first_change(times, width, case["up"], at_up, STEP_PT + 1e-6)
+    if began is None:
+        return None
+    t, v = shares(times, width, began, min(case["end"], case["up"] + 1.0), at_up, 0.0)
+    spring = fit_raw(t, v)
+    return {"delay": float(began - case["up"] - reach(spring, STEP_PT / at_up)), "seen_ms": float((began - case["up"]) * 1000), **spring}
+
+
+def glow_fit(case):
+    times, rows = case["times"], case["rows"]
+    first = rest_index(times, case["down"])
+    glow = np.array(rows["glow"]) - rows["glow"][first]
+    f = case["features"]
+    peak, cut = f["glow_peak"], f["glow_drop_ms"]
+    if peak < press.MIN_GLOW:
+        return None
+    hold_level = f["glow_drop"] if cut is not None else 0.0
+    swell = peak - hold_level
+    during = (times >= case["down"]) & (times <= case["up"])
+    t, v = shares(times, glow, case["down"], case["up"], 0.0, peak, zero=case["down"])
+    rise = fit_jump(t, v)
+    if cut is None:
+        return {"peak": float(peak), "hold_level": 0.0, "swell": float(peak), "first_share": None, "rise": rise, "hold_ms": None, "fade": None, "cut_ms": None}
+    after = (times > case["up"]) & (times < case["up"] + cut / 1000.0)
+    held = None
+    for t_, g in zip(times[after], glow[after]):
+        if g < peak - HOLD_DROP:
+            held = t_ - case["up"]
+            break
+    fade = None
+    if held is not None and swell > press.MIN_GLOW / 2:
+        t, v = shares(times, glow, case["up"] + held, case["up"] + cut / 1000.0 - 1e-6, peak, hold_level)
+        fade = fit_raw(t, v)
+    return {
+        "peak": float(peak),
+        "hold_level": float(hold_level),
+        "swell": float(swell),
+        "first_share": float(glow[during][0] / peak) if during.any() else 0.0,
+        "rise": rise,
+        "hold_ms": None if held is None else float(held * 1000),
+        "fade": fade,
+        "cut_ms": float(cut),
+    }
+
+
+def median(values):
+    values = [v for v in values if v is not None and np.isfinite(v)]
+    return float(np.median(values)) if values else None
+
+
+def gaussian_mean(width, height, sigma, focus=(0.0, 0.0), shape="capsule"):
+    xs = np.linspace(-width / 2, width / 2, 121)
+    ys = np.linspace(-height / 2, height / 2, 61)
+    x, y = np.meshgrid(xs, ys)
+    inside = inside_shape(x, y, width, height, shape)
+    values = np.exp(-((x - focus[0]) ** 2 + (y - focus[1]) ** 2) / (2 * sigma * sigma))
+    return float(values[inside].mean())
+
+
+def inside_shape(x, y, width, height, shape):
+    if shape == "circle":
+        return x * x + y * y <= (width / 2) ** 2
+    radius = min(width, height) / 2 if shape == "capsule" else 32.0
+    cx = np.clip(np.abs(x), 0, width / 2 - radius)
+    cy = np.clip(np.abs(y), 0, height / 2 - radius)
+    return (np.abs(x) - cx) ** 2 + (np.abs(y) - cy) ** 2 <= radius * radius
+
+
+def fit_sigma(profile, hold_level):
+    distances = np.array([p[0] for p in profile])
+    values = np.array([p[1] for p in profile]) - hold_level
+    best = (math.inf, None, None)
+    for sigma in SIGMAS:
+        shape = np.exp(-(distances**2) / (2 * sigma * sigma))
+        scale = float((shape * values).sum() / (shape * shape).sum())
+        error = float(np.sqrt(np.mean((scale * shape - values) ** 2)))
+        if error < best[0]:
+            best = (error, float(sigma), scale)
+    return {"sigma": best[1], "peak": best[2], "rms": best[0], "on_edge": best[1] in (SIGMAS[0], SIGMAS[-1])}
+
+
+def size_table(fits, sigma):
+    by_size = {}
+    for case, fit in fits:
+        width, height, shape = case["size"]
+        by_size.setdefault((width, height, shape), []).append(fit)
+    table = []
+    for (width, height, shape), found in sorted(by_size.items(), key=lambda item: item[0][0] * item[0][1]):
+        hold = median([f["hold_level"] for f in found])
+        swell = median([f["swell"] for f in found])
+        mean = gaussian_mean(width, height, sigma, shape=shape)
+        table.append({"width": width, "height": height, "shape": shape, "area": width * height, "hold": hold, "swell": swell, "swell_peak": swell / mean, "cut_ms": median([f["cut_ms"] for f in found]), "cases": len(found)})
+    measured = [row for row in table if row["cut_ms"] is not None]
+    for row in table:
+        if row["cut_ms"] is None and measured:
+            nearest = min(measured, key=lambda other: abs(math.log(other["area"]) - math.log(row["area"])))
+            row["cut_ms"] = nearest["cut_ms"]
+            row["cut_from"] = [nearest["width"], nearest["height"]]
+    return table
+
+
+def edge_share(fits):
+    flags = [bool(fit.get("at_grid_edge")) for fit in fits if fit]
+    return (sum(flags) / len(flags)) if flags else 0.0
+
+
+def fit(series, profile=None):
+    cases = list(presses(series))
+    normal = [c for c in cases if c["mode"] == "normal"]
+    photo = [c for c in normal if c["backdrop"] == "photo"]
+    growth = [g for g in (growth_fit(c) for c in normal) if g]
+    release = [r for r in (release_fit(c) for c in normal) if r]
+    glows = [(c, g) for c, g in ((c, glow_fit(c)) for c in cases) if g]
+    normal_glows = [g for c, g in glows if c["mode"] == "normal"]
+    reduce_glows = [g for c, g in glows if c["mode"] == "reduce-motion"]
+    sigma = fit_sigma(profile["profile"], profile["hold_level"]) if profile else {"sigma": None}
+    per_take = [g for g in growth] + [r for r in release] + [g["rise"] for g in normal_glows if g["rise"]] + [g["fade"] for g in normal_glows if g["fade"]]
+    summary = {
+        "skipped": skipped(series),
+        "held_out": list(HELD_OUT),
+        "edge_share": edge_share(per_take),
+        "law": fit_growth_law(list(plateaus(series))),
+        "growth": {"delay": median([g["delay"] for g in growth]), "response": median([g["response"] for g in growth]), "damping": median([g["damping"] for g in growth]), "cases": len(growth)},
+        "release": {"delay": median([r["delay"] for r in release]), "response": median([r["response"] for r in release]), "damping": median([r["damping"] for r in release]), "cases": len(release)},
+        "glow": {
+            "rise_jump": median([g["rise"]["jump"] for g in normal_glows if g["rise"]]),
+            "rise_response": median([g["rise"]["response"] for g in normal_glows if g["rise"]]),
+            "rise_damping": median([g["rise"]["damping"] for g in normal_glows if g["rise"]]),
+            "hold_ms": median([g["hold_ms"] for g in normal_glows]),
+            "fade_response": median([g["fade"]["response"] for g in normal_glows if g["fade"]]),
+            "fade_damping": median([g["fade"]["damping"] for g in normal_glows if g["fade"]]),
+            "cut_ms": median([g["cut_ms"] for g in normal_glows]),
+            "reduce_motion_cut_ms": median([g["cut_ms"] for g in reduce_glows]),
+            "reduce_motion_presses": sum(1 for g in reduce_glows if g["cut_ms"] is not None),
+            "cut_ms_range": [min(g["cut_ms"] for g in normal_glows if g["cut_ms"]), max(g["cut_ms"] for g in normal_glows if g["cut_ms"])] if normal_glows else None,
+            "cases": len(normal_glows),
+            "reduce_motion_cases": len(reduce_glows),
+        },
+        "sigma": sigma,
+    }
+    if sigma["sigma"]:
+        summary["sizes"] = size_table([(c, g) for c, g in glows if c["mode"] == "normal"], sigma["sigma"])
+    return summary
+
+
+def problems(summary):
+    found = []
+    if summary["law"]["on_edge"]:
+        found.append("growth law on its grid edge")
+    if not summary["law"].get("within", True):
+        found.append(f"no growth law form keeps every measured height within {SIZE_LIMIT} pt")
+    for part in ("growth", "release"):
+        if summary[part]["response"] is None:
+            found.append(f"{part}: no fit")
+    glow = summary["glow"]
+    for key in ("rise_response", "hold_ms", "fade_response", "cut_ms", "reduce_motion_cut_ms"):
+        if glow[key] is None:
+            found.append(f"glow {key}: no fit")
+    if not summary.get("sizes"):
+        found.append("no glow size table")
+    if summary.get("edge_share", 0.0) > EDGE_SHARE:
+        found.append(f"{summary['edge_share']:.0%} of the per-take spring fits sit on the grid edge (limit {EDGE_SHARE:.0%})")
+    if summary["glow"].get("reduce_motion_presses", 0) < REDUCE_MOTION_PRESSES:
+        found.append(f"the Reduce Motion cut rests on {summary['glow'].get('reduce_motion_presses', 0)} presses (need {REDUCE_MOTION_PRESSES})")
+    if summary["sigma"].get("on_edge"):
+        found.append("glow sigma on its grid edge")
+    return found
+
+
+def number(value):
+    return f"{float(value)}"
+
+
+def table_source(summary):
+    law, growth, release, glow = summary["law"], summary["growth"], summary["release"], summary["glow"]
+    sizes = summary["sizes"]
+    lines = [
+        f"const double ios27PressGrowthCap = {number(law['cap'])};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGrowthAt100 = {number(law['at_100'])};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGrowthPower = {number(law['power'])};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGrowthWidthPower = {number(law.get('width_power', 0.0))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGrowthDelay = {number(round(growth['delay'], 4))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGrowthResponse = {number(round(growth['response'], 3))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGrowthDamping = {number(round(growth['damping'], 3))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressReleaseDelay = {number(round(release['delay'], 4))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressReleaseResponse = {number(round(release['response'], 3))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressReleaseDamping = {number(round(release['damping'], 3))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGlowRiseJump = {number(round(glow['rise_jump'], 3))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGlowRiseResponse = {number(round(glow['rise_response'], 3))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGlowRiseDamping = {number(round(glow['rise_damping'], 3))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGlowHold = {number(round(glow['hold_ms'] / 1000, 4))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGlowFadeResponse = {number(round(glow['fade_response'], 3))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGlowFadeDamping = {number(round(glow['fade_damping'], 3))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressReduceMotionGlowCut = {number(round(glow['reduce_motion_cut_ms'] / 1000, 4))};",
+        f"const double ios27PressGlowSigma = {number(summary['sigma']['sigma'])};",
+        "",
+        "const List<double> ios27PressGlowAreas = [" + ", ".join(number(round(s["area"], 2)) for s in sizes) + "];",
+        "const List<double> ios27PressGlowHoldLuma = [" + ", ".join(number(round(s["hold"], 3)) for s in sizes) + "];",
+        "const List<double> ios27PressGlowSwellLuma = [" + ", ".join(number(round(s["swell_peak"], 3)) for s in sizes) + "];",
+        "const List<double> ios27PressGlowCutSeconds = [" + ", ".join(number(round(s["cut_ms"] / 1000, 4)) for s in sizes) + "];",
+    ]
+    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
+
+
+def write_table(summary, target=None):
+    found = problems(summary)
+    target = Path(target or TABLE)
+    if found:
+        raise SystemExit(f"pressfit --write refused, nothing written to {target}:\n" + "\n".join(f"  - {p}" for p in found))
+    target.write_text(table_source(summary))
+    return target
+
+
+def rings(change, width_pt, height_pt, shape, scale):
+    height, width = change.shape
+    ys, xs = np.mgrid[0:height, 0:width]
+    x = (xs + 0.5) / scale - width / scale / 2
+    y = (ys + 0.5) / scale - height / scale / 2
+    inside = inside_shape(x, y, width_pt, height_pt, shape)
+    distance = np.hypot(x, y)
+    bins = np.floor(distance).astype(int)
+    return [(float(b) + 0.5, float(change[(bins == b) & inside].mean())) for b in range(int(bins.max()) + 1) if ((bins == b) & inside).sum() >= 9]
+
+
+def median_profile(profiles):
+    rings = {}
+    for profile in profiles:
+        for distance, value in profile["profile"]:
+            rings.setdefault(distance, []).append(value)
+    count = len(profiles)
+    return {
+        "takes": [p["take"] for p in profiles],
+        "box_pt": profiles[0]["box_pt"],
+        "shape": profiles[0]["shape"],
+        "profile": [(distance, float(np.median(values))) for distance, values in sorted(rings.items()) if len(values) == count],
+        "hold_level": float(np.median([p["hold_level"] for p in profiles])),
+    }
+
+
+def profile_take(runs, key):
+    scene, case, run = key.split("/")
+    return Path(runs) / run / scene / case / "native"
+
+
+def glow_profile(take, region, hold_level, shape="capsule"):
+    import metrics
+    import touch
+    import track
+
+    take = Path(take)
+    px = track.pixel_rect(region)
+    bare = track.crop_px(metrics.load(take / "bare" / "ready.png"), px)
+    full = track.crop_px(metrics.load(take / "ready.png"), px)
+    left, top, right, bottom = track.box_pixels(full, bare, track.edges(bare))
+    frames = track.extract(take / "video.mp4", px, take / "profile")
+    down, up = touch.read(take / "video.mp4", take / "marker")[0]
+    rest = rest_index(frames.times, down)
+    held = min(range(len(frames.times)), key=lambda i: abs(frames.times[i] - (down + up) / 2))
+    change = (metrics.luma(frames[held]) - metrics.luma(frames[rest]))[top : bottom + 1, left : right + 1]
+    width_pt, height_pt = (right - left + 1) / metrics.SCALE, (bottom - top + 1) / metrics.SCALE
+    return {"take": str(take), "box_pt": [width_pt, height_pt], "shape": shape, "profile": rings(change, width_pt, height_pt, shape, metrics.SCALE), "hold_level": float(hold_level)}
+
+
+def main(argv=None):
+    import argparse
+
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Fit the press model (size law, growth and release springs, glow) on native press series.")
+    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    profile = commands.add_parser("profile", help="read the glow's spatial profile from a native Reduce Motion material.interactive take")
+    profile.add_argument("keys", nargs="+", help="scene/case/run keys of native Reduce Motion takes in the series")
+    profile.add_argument("--series", required=True)
+    profile.add_argument("--runs", required=True)
+    profile.add_argument("--out", required=True)
+    fitting = commands.add_parser("fit", help="fit the model on series written by native_questions.py and a saved profile")
+    fitting.add_argument("series", nargs="+")
+    fitting.add_argument("--profile", required=True)
+    fitting.add_argument("--out")
+    fitting.add_argument("--write", action="store_true")
+    options = parser.parse_args(argv)
+    if options.command == "profile":
+        series = json.loads(Path(options.series).read_text())
+        found = median_profile([glow_profile(profile_take(options.runs, key), (46, 377, 310, 148), series[key]["steps"]["step1"]["glass"]["glow_drop"]) for key in options.keys])
+        Path(options.out).write_text(json.dumps(found, indent=1))
+        print(f"median profile of {len(found['takes'])} takes, {len(found['profile'])} rings, written to {options.out}")
+        return
+    summary = fit(load(options.series), json.loads(Path(options.profile).read_text()))
+    text = json.dumps(summary, indent=1)
+    if options.out:
+        Path(options.out).write_text(text + "\n")
+    print(text)
+    for problem in problems(summary):
+        print("PROBLEM", problem)
+    if options.write:
+        print(f"written {write_table(summary)}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
```

### Task 5: M7: interactive glass presses like native (scale, light, cut per size, release, Reduce Motion, touches)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/glass_press.dart`, `glass_press_widgets.dart` (with `debugGlassPress`, exported for tests), `ios27_press.dart` (written by `pressfit.py fit --write`)
- Modify: `packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/glass_motion_coordinator.dart`, `glass_shape_motion.dart` (`contentScale`), `lib/src/liquid_glass.dart` (content scaled about its centre), `lib/src/api/glass_effect.dart` (the press target and light on interactive glass), `lib/ios_liquid_glass.dart` (export)
- Test: `packages/ios_liquid_glass/test/motion/glass_press_test.dart`; `glass_group_test.dart`, `glass_union_test.dart`, `render_hooks_test.dart` (their fake motions gain `contentScale`)

**Why.** Rulings 3 to 13. The press is the member's own state, sampled on the coordinator's ticker, so no widget rebuilds while glass is pressed. The drawn rect and the content scale together about the centre. The target listens to raw pointer events and hit-tests the glass's shape, so it never enters the gesture arena; the first target to see a pointer down claims it and a hit-test path is dispatched innermost first, so only the innermost interactive glass reacts.

```diff
+
+const Duration _frame = Duration(milliseconds: 16);
+
+Duration _seconds(double value) => Duration(microseconds: (value * 1000000).round());
+
+Widget _app(Widget child, {bool reduceMotion = false}) => MaterialApp(
+  home: MediaQuery(
+    data: MediaQueryData(disableAnimations: reduceMotion),
+    child: Center(child: child),
+  ),
+);
+
+Widget _block({Glass? glass, double width = 250, double height = 88, Key? key, Widget? child}) => GlassEffect(
+  key: key,
+  glass: glass ?? Glass.regular.interactive(),
+  child: SizedBox(width: width, height: height, child: child),
+);
+
+List<GlassMember> _members(WidgetTester tester) =>
+    tester.renderObjectList<RenderGlassMemberBox>(find.byType(GlassMemberBox)).map((box) => box.member).toList();
+
+GlassMember _member(WidgetTester tester) => _members(tester).single;
+
+Future<void> _show(WidgetTester tester, Widget widget) async {
+  await tester.pumpWidget(widget);
+  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 2));
+}
+
+Future<void> _hold(WidgetTester tester, Duration duration) async {
+  final end = tester.binding.clock.now().add(duration);
+  while (tester.binding.clock.now().isBefore(end)) {
+    await tester.pump(_frame);
+  }
+}

+    expect(GlassPress.widthGrowth(const Size(360, 200)), closeTo(law(360, 200), 1e-9));
```

```diff
diff --git a/packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/glass_press.dart b/packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/glass_press.dart
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..037a33a01e3c5353afa0b69c21476c647685e6e8
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/glass_press.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,159 @@
+import 'dart:math' as math;
+import 'dart:ui';
+
+import 'package:ios_liquid_glass/src/motion/glass_animation.dart';
+import 'package:ios_liquid_glass/src/motion/glass_spring.dart';
+import 'package:ios_liquid_glass/src/motion/ios27_press.dart';
+import 'package:meta/meta.dart';
+
+@internal
+class GlassPress {
+  final GlassSpring _growth = GlassSpring(0);
+  final GlassSpring _swell = GlassSpring(0);
+  final GlassSpring _focusX = GlassSpring(0);
+  final GlassSpring _focusY = GlassSpring(0);
+  bool _pressed = false;
+  bool _lit = false;
+  bool _reduceMotion = false;
+  double _cut = 0;
+  Duration? _downAt;
+  Duration? _upAt;
+  bool _growthStarted = false;
+  bool _releaseStarted = false;
+  bool _fadeStarted = false;
+  bool _downPending = false;
+  bool _upPending = false;
+
+  static const GlassAnimation growthAnimation =
+      GlassAnimation.dampedSpring(response: ios27PressGrowthResponse, dampingFraction: ios27PressGrowthDamping);
+  static const GlassAnimation releaseAnimation =
+      GlassAnimation.dampedSpring(response: ios27PressReleaseResponse, dampingFraction: ios27PressReleaseDamping);
+  static const GlassAnimation riseAnimation =
+      GlassAnimation.dampedSpring(response: ios27PressGlowRiseResponse, dampingFraction: ios27PressGlowRiseDamping);
+  static const GlassAnimation fadeAnimation =
+      GlassAnimation.dampedSpring(response: ios27PressGlowFadeResponse, dampingFraction: ios27PressGlowFadeDamping);
+  static const GlassAnimation followAnimation = GlassAnimation.dampedSpring(response: 1, dampingFraction: 1);
+
+  bool get isPressed => _pressed;
+
+  bool get isActive => _pressed || _lit || _growth.isMoving || _growth.value != 0 || _swell.isMoving || _focusX.isMoving || _focusY.isMoving;
+
+  double get growth => _growth.value;
+
+  double get hold => _lit ? 1 : 0;
+
+  double get swell => _lit ? _swell.value.clamp(0.0, 1.0) : 0;
+
+  Offset get focus => Offset(_focusX.value, _focusY.value);
+
+  static double widthGrowth(Size size) {
+    if (size.height <= 0 || size.width <= 0) return 0;
+    return math.min(ios27PressGrowthCap, ios27PressGrowthAt100 * math.pow(100 / size.height, ios27PressGrowthPower) * math.pow(100 / size.width, ios27PressGrowthWidthPower));
+  }
+
+  static double scaleFor(Size size, double growth) {
+    if (size.width <= 0 || growth == 0) return 1;
+    return 1 + widthGrowth(size) / size.width * growth;
+  }
+
+  static (double, double) glowFor(Size size) {
+    final area = size.width * size.height;
+    return (_interpolate(area, ios27PressGlowHoldLuma) / 255, _interpolate(area, ios27PressGlowSwellLuma) / 255);
+  }
+
+  static double cutFor(Size size, {required bool reduceMotion}) =>
+      reduceMotion ? ios27PressReduceMotionGlowCut : _interpolate(size.width * size.height, ios27PressGlowCutSeconds);
+
+  static double _interpolate(double area, List<double> values) {
+    const areas = ios27PressGlowAreas;
+    if (area <= areas.first) return values.first;
+    if (area >= areas.last) return values.last;
+    for (var i = 1; i < areas.length; i++) {
+      if (area <= areas[i]) {
+        final a = math.log(areas[i - 1]), b = math.log(areas[i]);
+        final t = (math.log(area) - a) / (b - a);
+        return values[i - 1] + (values[i] - values[i - 1]) * t;
+      }
+    }
+    return values.last;
+  }
+
+  void down(Offset position, {required bool reduceMotion, required double cut, Duration? now}) {
+    _pressed = true;
+    _cut = cut;
+    _lit = true;
+    _reduceMotion = reduceMotion;
+    _downAt = now;
+    _downPending = now == null;
+    _upAt = null;
+    _upPending = false;
+    _growthStarted = false;
+    _releaseStarted = false;
+    _fadeStarted = false;
+    _focusX.jumpTo(position.dx);
+    _focusY.jumpTo(position.dy);
+    _swell.restart(ios27PressGlowRiseJump, 0, 1, riseAnimation, now);
+  }
+
+  void move(Offset position, {Duration? now}) {
+    if (!_pressed) return;
+    _focusX.animateTo(position.dx, followAnimation, now);
+    _focusY.animateTo(position.dy, followAnimation, now);
+  }
+
+  void up({Duration? now}) {
+    if (!_pressed) return;
+    _pressed = false;
+    _upAt = now;
+    _upPending = now == null;
+  }
+
+  bool sample(Duration now) {
+    if (_downPending) {
+      _downPending = false;
+      _downAt = now;
+    }
+    if (_upPending) {
+      _upPending = false;
+      _upAt = now;
+    }
+    final down = _downAt;
+    if (_pressed && down != null && !_growthStarted && !_reduceMotion && now - down >= _seconds(ios27PressGrowthDelay)) {
+      _growthStarted = true;
+      _growth.animateTo(1, growthAnimation, now);
+    }
+    final up = _upAt;
+    if (!_pressed && up != null) {
+      final since = now - up;
+      if (!_releaseStarted && since >= _seconds(ios27PressReleaseDelay)) {
+        _releaseStarted = true;
+        _growthStarted = true;
+        _growth.animateTo(0, releaseAnimation, now);
+      }
+      if (!_fadeStarted && since >= _seconds(ios27PressGlowHold)) {
+        _fadeStarted = true;
+        _swell.animateTo(0, fadeAnimation, now);
+      }
+      if (_lit && since >= _seconds(_cut)) {
+        _lit = false;
+        _swell.jumpTo(0);
+        _upAt = null;
+      }
+    }
+    var moving = _growth.sample(now);
+    moving = _swell.sample(now) || moving;
+    moving = _focusX.sample(now) || moving;
+    moving = _focusY.sample(now) || moving;
+    return moving || _pressed || _lit;
+  }
+
+  void settle() {
+    _pressed = false;
+    _lit = false;
+    _upAt = null;
+    _growth.jumpTo(0);
+    _swell.jumpTo(0);
+  }
+
+  static Duration _seconds(double value) => Duration(microseconds: (value * Duration.microsecondsPerSecond).round());
+}
diff --git a/packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/glass_press_widgets.dart b/packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/glass_press_widgets.dart
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..5d7be13eae6b383bc1dd5a22b94892f1056a387b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/glass_press_widgets.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,176 @@
+import 'dart:math' as math;
+
+import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
+import 'package:flutter/rendering.dart';
+import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
+import 'package:ios_liquid_glass/src/liquid_shape.dart';
+import 'package:ios_liquid_glass/src/motion/glass_motion_coordinator.dart';
+import 'package:ios_liquid_glass/src/motion/glass_motion_widgets.dart';
+import 'package:ios_liquid_glass/src/motion/glass_press.dart';
+import 'package:ios_liquid_glass/src/motion/ios27_press.dart';
+
+@internal
+class GlassPressTarget extends SingleChildRenderObjectWidget {
+  const GlassPressTarget({super.key, required this.member, required this.shape, this.scrolling, super.child});
+
+  final GlassMember member;
+  final LiquidShape shape;
+  final ScrollPosition? scrolling;
+
+  @override
+  RenderGlassPressTarget createRenderObject(BuildContext context) => RenderGlassPressTarget(member: member, shape: shape, scrolling: scrolling);
+
+  @override
+  void updateRenderObject(BuildContext context, RenderGlassPressTarget renderObject) {
+    renderObject
+      ..member = member
+      ..shape = shape
+      ..scrolling = scrolling;
+  }
+}
+
+@internal
+class RenderGlassPressTarget extends RenderProxyBoxWithHitTestBehavior {
+  RenderGlassPressTarget({required GlassMember member, required this.shape, ScrollPosition? scrolling})
+    : _member = member,
+      _scrolling = scrolling,
+      super(behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque);
+
+  static final Map<int, RenderGlassPressTarget> _owners = {};
+
+  @visibleForTesting
+  static Iterable<int> get claimedPointers => _owners.keys;
+
+  GlassMember _member;
+  LiquidShape shape;
+  ScrollPosition? _scrolling;
+  int? _pointer;
+  double? _pixelsAtDown;
+
+  GlassMember get member => _member;
+  set member(GlassMember value) {
+    if (identical(value, _member)) return;
+    _release();
+    _member = value;
+  }
+
+  set scrolling(ScrollPosition? value) {
+    if (identical(value, _scrolling)) return;
+    if (attached) _scrolling?.removeListener(_scrolled);
+    _scrolling = value;
+    if (attached) _scrolling?.addListener(_scrolled);
+  }
+
+  @override
+  void attach(PipelineOwner owner) {
+    super.attach(owner);
+    _scrolling?.addListener(_scrolled);
+  }
+
+  @override
+  void detach() {
+    _scrolling?.removeListener(_scrolled);
+    super.detach();
+  }
+
+  @override
+  void dispose() {
+    _release();
+    super.dispose();
+  }
+
+  void _scrolled() {
+    final position = _scrolling;
+    if (position == null || _pointer == null || !position.hasPixels) return;
+    if (position.pixels != _pixelsAtDown) _release();
+  }
+
+  @override
+  bool hitTest(BoxHitTestResult result, {required Offset position}) {
+    if (!size.contains(position) || !shape.getOuterPath(Offset.zero & size).contains(position)) return false;
+    return super.hitTest(result, position: position);
+  }
+
+  @override
+  void handleEvent(PointerEvent event, BoxHitTestEntry entry) {
+    if (event is PointerDownEvent) {
+      if (_pointer != null || _owners.containsKey(event.pointer)) return;
+      _owners[event.pointer] = this;
+      _pointer = event.pointer;
+      final position = _scrolling;
+      _pixelsAtDown = position != null && position.hasPixels ? position.pixels : null;
+      _member.pressDown(event.localPosition);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (event.pointer != _pointer) return;
+    if (event is PointerMoveEvent) {
+      _member.pressMove(event.localPosition);
+    } else if (event is PointerUpEvent || event is PointerCancelEvent) {
+      _release();
+    }
+  }
+
+  void _release() {
+    final pointer = _pointer;
+    if (pointer == null) return;
+    _pointer = null;
+    if (identical(_owners[pointer], this)) _owners.remove(pointer);
+    _member.pressUp();
+  }
+}
+
+@internal
+class GlassPressGlow extends CustomPainter {
+  GlassPressGlow(this.member) : super(repaint: member);
+
+  final GlassMember member;
+
+  static const List<double> _stops = [0, 0.125, 0.25, 0.375, 0.5, 0.625, 0.75, 0.875, 1];
+
+  @override
+  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
+    final press = member.press;
+    final hold = press.hold, swell = press.swell;
+    if (hold == 0 && swell == 0) return;
+    final (holdAlpha, swellAlpha) = GlassPress.glowFor(size);
+    final rect = Offset.zero & size;
+    if (hold > 0 && holdAlpha > 0) {
+      canvas.drawRect(rect, Paint()
+        ..color = Color.fromRGBO(255, 255, 255, (holdAlpha * hold).clamp(0.0, 1.0))
+        ..blendMode = BlendMode.plus);
+    }
+    if (swell > 0 && swellAlpha > 0) {
+      const reach = 3.0;
+      final radius = ios27PressGlowSigma * reach;
+      final peak = (swellAlpha * swell).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
+      canvas.drawRect(rect, Paint()
+        ..shader = RadialGradient(
+          colors: [for (final stop in _stops) Color.fromRGBO(255, 255, 255, peak * math.exp(-(stop * reach) * (stop * reach) / 2))],
+          stops: _stops,
+        ).createShader(Rect.fromCircle(center: press.focus, radius: radius))
+        ..blendMode = BlendMode.plus);
+    }
+  }
+
+  @override
+  bool shouldRepaint(GlassPressGlow oldDelegate) => !identical(oldDelegate.member, member);
+}
+
+@visibleForTesting
+({bool pressed, double scale, double light}) debugGlassPress(Element element) {
+  RenderGlassMemberBox? found;
+  void visit(Element child) {
+    if (found != null) return;
+    final render = child.renderObject;
+    if (child is RenderObjectElement && render is RenderGlassMemberBox) {
+      found = render;
+      return;
+    }
+    child.visitChildren(visit);
+  }
+
+  element.visitChildren(visit);
+  final member = found?.member;
+  if (member == null) return (pressed: false, scale: 1, light: 0);
+  return (pressed: member.press.isPressed, scale: member.contentScale, light: member.press.hold + member.press.swell);
+}

diff --git a/packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/ios27_press.dart b/packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/ios27_press.dart
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2cf58d28e62e81b1bfb84ce385cc0585a87f61b3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/mobile/packages/ios_liquid_glass/lib/src/motion/ios27_press.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,23 @@
+const double ios27PressGrowthCap = 15.84;
+const double ios27PressGrowthAt100 = 9.73;
+const double ios27PressGrowthPower = 1.24;
+const double ios27PressGrowthWidthPower = 0.0;
+const double ios27PressGrowthDelay = 0.0513;
+const double ios27PressGrowthResponse = 0.27;
+const double ios27PressGrowthDamping = 0.62;
+const double ios27PressReleaseDelay = 0.0207;
+const double ios27PressReleaseResponse = 0.33;
+const double ios27PressReleaseDamping = 0.59;
+const double ios27PressGlowRiseJump = 0.0;
+const double ios27PressGlowRiseResponse = 0.04;
+const double ios27PressGlowRiseDamping = 1.845;
+const double ios27PressGlowHold = 0.0833;
+const double ios27PressGlowFadeResponse = 0.31;
+const double ios27PressGlowFadeDamping = 1.35;
+const double ios27PressReduceMotionGlowCut = 0.5642;
+const double ios27PressGlowSigma = 77.0;
+
+const List<double> ios27PressGlowAreas = [3364.0, 7314.0, 11000.0, 22000.0, 36000.0, 37500.0, 72000.0, 75000.0];
+const List<double> ios27PressGlowHoldLuma = [9.789, 11.34, 11.09, 11.227, 11.052, 10.819, 0.0, 0.0];
+const List<double> ios27PressGlowSwellLuma = [32.632, 11.135, 7.941, 11.51, 9.98, 11.648, 9.718, 13.565];
+const List<double> ios27PressGlowCutSeconds = [0.6517, 0.525, 0.4183, 0.4, 0.3808, 0.3717, 0.3717, 0.3717];
```

### Task 8: M10: `perf.motion` and `perf.slide`

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/ios_liquid_glass/example/lib/lab/scenes/perf_scenes.dart`

**Why.** Ruling 14. `perf.motion` keeps glass animating every frame: press pulses on four interactive glasses (synthetic pointer events every 400 ms), three glasses that materialize and dematerialize, a container whose three glasses merge and split, and a row that slides by a sub-pixel amount every frame (the A9 re-raster). `perf.slide` slides `perf.material`'s whole layout. `perf.material`'s widget tree is unchanged.
